                player_battle_sprite(l).y = (player_battle_sprite(l).y + 4) - l
            End If
        Next
    Next
    If isFleeing = 0 Then
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 1
        Call PAL_Delay(draw_battle_scene(0, 1), draw_text_at(14, 31, 90, 128), 22)
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).origDirection
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).origX
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).origY
        playerExp(roleID, 7).count = playerExp(roleID, 7).count + 2
    Else
        battle_select_max = -1
    End If
    Call draw_battle_scene(-4, 1)
    If isFleeing Then
        isFleeing = 2
    End If
    battle_action_param = isFleeing
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub check_save_file()
    For i = 1 To 5
        bstr = VB40032.rtcBstrFromAnsi(48 + i)
        filename = VB40032.rtcBstrFromAnsi(48 + i) & '.RPG'
        openResult = open_file(0, filename)
        If openResult > 0 Then
            Call kernel32._hread(openResult, battle_order_array(i - 1), 2)
            If battle_order_array(i - 1) > max_save_number Then
                max_save_number = battle_order_array(i - 1)
            End If
        Else
            battle_order_array(i - 1) = 0
        End If
        Call kernel32._lclose(openResult)
    Next
    year = -1
    month = -2
    day = 0
    While month = -2
        If day < 0 Then
            day = 0
        End If
        If day > 4 Then
            day = 4
        End If
        retryCount = 4
        For i = 0 To 4
            Call frame_menu(year, 6, retryCount, 180)
            If i = day Then
                Call draw_text_at(250, 43 + i, retryCount + 9, 197)
            Else
                Call draw_string(0, 3, word_dat_data(43 + i), retryCount + 10, 198)
            End If
            Call display_number(0, battle_order_array(i), retryCount + 15, 270)
            retryCount = retryCount + 38
        Next
        year = 0
        fileResult = read_key()
        If fileResult = 1 Then
            month = -1
        End If
        If fileResult = 3 Then
            day = day - 1
        End If
        If fileResult = 4 Then
            day = day + 1
        End If
        If fileResult = 2 Then
            month = day
        End If
    Wend
    retval = month
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_all_kinds_music(ByRef musicNum, ByRef loopFlag)
    If music_mode Then
        Call midi_close()
        Call cd_stop()
        If use_cd_flag Then
            If (musicNum > 0) And (musicNum <> 29) Then
                tmp3 = vb_form_ref
                tmp4 = trim_string(tmp3)
                cmd = tmp4
                cmd = cmd & '\\MUSICS\\'
                If musicNum < 10 Then
                    cmd = cmd & '00'
                Else
                    cmd = cmd & '0'
                End If
                cmd = cmd + LTrim(CStr(musicNum))
                cmd = cmd & '.MID'
                tmp4 = 'open ' & cmd
                cmdStr = ('open ' & cmd) & ' type sequencer alias midi'
                tmp3 = tmp
                mciResult = kernel32.mciSendStringA(tmp4, tmp5, 30, 0)
                tmp2 = mciResult
                tmp5 = tmp
                mciResult = kernel32.mciSendStringA(tmp6, tmp4, 30, 0)
                tmp2 = mciResult
                midi_playing = loopFlag And 1
                midi_active = 1
                music_mode_arg = midi_playing
                music_track_arg = musicNum
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub read_direction_input(ByRef dx, ByRef dy)
    key_pressed = check_key_pressed()
    dirChanged = 0
    newDir = 0
    dx = 0
    dy = 0
    For tmp = 2 To 3
        keyResult = 4 - tmp
        If (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(0)) = tmp) Or (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(4)) = tmp) Then
            dirChanged = keyResult
        End If
        If (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(1)) = tmp) Or (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(5)) = tmp) Then
            newDir = keyResult
        End If
        If (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(2)) = tmp) Or (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(6)) = tmp) Then
            dx = keyResult
        End If
        If (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(3)) = tmp) Or (key_state_array(key_scan_codes(7)) = tmp) Then
            dy = keyResult
        End If
    Next
    If (dirChanged + newDir) = 0 Then
        keyPressed = 0
        If dx > dy Then
            retval = -1
        End If
        If dx < dy Then
            retval = 1
        End If
    End If
    If (dx + dy) = 0 Then
        retval = 0
        If dirChanged > newDir Then
            keyPressed = -1
        End If
        If dirChanged < newDir Then
            keyPressed = 1
        End If
    End If
    If dirChanged = 2 Then
        keyPressed = -1
        retval = 0
    End If
    If newDir = 2 Then
        keyPressed = 1
        retval = 0
    End If
    If dx = 2 Then
        retval = -1
        keyPressed = 0
    End If
    If dy = 2 Then
        retval = 1
        keyPressed = 0
    End If
    If (dy = 0) And (retval = 1) Then
        retval = 0
    End If
    If (dx = 0) And (retval = -1) Then
        retval = 0
    End If
    If (newDir = 0) And (keyPressed = 1) Then
        keyPressed = 0
    End If
    If (dirChanged = 0) And (keyPressed = -1) Then
        keyPressed = 0
    End If
    dx = retval
    dy = keyPressed
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_opening_anim()
    Call PAL_Unpak(load_fbp_two_scene(61), load_subfile_to_buf(571), fire_mkf_data(0), global_buf_2(0))
    Call PAL_Unpak(load_subfile_to_buf(572), mgo_frame_offsets(0), global_buf_2(0))
    k = 0
    x = 220
    y = -400
    i = 200
    color = 0
    For j = 0 To 399
        i = i - (j And 1)
        If i >= 0 Then
            bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
            offset = bufPtr + (i * 320)
        End If
        Call PAL_ClearClipNA(global_buf_2(0), offset, viewport_offset, viewport_height, viewport_offset, 0, 0)
        Call PAL_RripA(global_buf_2(0), 2, viewport_offset)
        Call PAL_PutIpNA(global_buf_2(0), fire_mkf_data(fire_mkf_data(0)), viewport_offset, 0, y, 0)
        Call PAL_PutIpNA(global_buf_2(0), fire_mkf_data(fire_mkf_data(1) And 32767), viewport_offset, 0, y + 200, 0)
        If y < 10 Then
            y = y + 1
        End If
        k = (k + 1) And 3
        x = x - (k And 1)
        If x < 80 Then
            x = 80
        End If
        Call PAL_PutIpNA(global_buf_2(0), mgo_frame_offsets(mgo_frame_offsets(k)), viewport_offset, 0, x, 230)
        If color <= 64 Then
            color = color + 1
            Call PAL_ExPalate(color, 720, palette_data(0), palette_data(768))
            Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
        End If
        Call PAL_NipWA(global_buf_2(0), viewport_offset, 0)
        Call PAL_WaitTime(5)
    Next
    palette_fade_active = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub timer_midi_cd_callback()  'MethCallEngine entry
    tmp4 = PAL_GetActiveWindow()
    If Me.method_0078(Me.method_0038(tmp4, tmp5, -) = tmp5, tmp13, -) And (tmp13 = 0) Then
        If exit_flag = 1 Then
            Call stop_app_and_music()
        End If
        If (cd_active = 1) And (cd_track_valid <> 0) Then
            If query_cd_status() = 0 Then
                tmp2 = (('play cdtrack from' + CStr(cd_track_num)) + ' to') + CStr(cd_track_num + 1)
                tmp15 = tmp
                tmp5 = kernel32.mciSendStringA(tmp14, tmp16, 30, 0)
                tmp3 = tmp5
            End If
        End If
        If (midi_active = 1) And (midi_playing = 1) Then
            If query_midi_status() = 0 Then
                tmp16 = tmp
                tmp5 = kernel32.mciSendStringA(tmp17, tmp15, 30, 0)
                tmp3 = tmp5
                tmp16 = tmp
                tmp5 = kernel32.mciSendStringA(tmp17, tmp15, 30, 0)
                tmp3 = tmp5
            End If
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If exit_flag = 0 Then
        Call stop_all_and_exit()
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_battle_scene(ByRef frameCount, ByRef speed)
    rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
    battle_dest_x = CSng (battle_dest_x) + (CSng (blow_away_flag) * rng)
    i = frameCount
    If i < 0 Then
        i = 1
    End If
    For magicIdx = 1 To frameCount
        rng = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
        Call PAL_ClearClipNA(global_buf_2(0), rng, dialog_y_pos, viewport_height, dialog_y_pos, battle_y_offset, 0)
        Call PAL_ExMyll(dialog_y_pos)
        partyX = theurgy_effect_count + theurgy_effect_max
        If partyX > 0 Then
            Call PAL_RripA(global_buf_2(0), partyX, dialog_y_pos)
        End If
        Call update_enemy_battle_anim()
        If npc_dir_frame Then
            Call draw_npc_sprite()
        End If
        Call PAL_NTree(draw_role_battle_frame(), draw_effect_sprites(0), update_damage_numbers(), global_buf_2(0), 0)
        Call PAL_NipWA(global_buf_2(0), dialog_y_pos, frame_counter)
        effect_sub_count = effect_sub_count + 1
        For j = 0 To team_number
            If player_hit_flags(j) Then
                spriteIdx = map_data_buf(enemy_battle_data(j).mapValue + enemy_battle_data(j).direction) + enemy_battle_data(j).mapValue
                yOffset = (enemy_battle_data(j).x - (map_data_buf(spriteIdx) \ 2)) + battle_dest_x
                frameDelay = (enemy_battle_data(j).y - map_data_buf(spriteIdx + 1)) + (battle_dest_x \ 2)
                Call PAL_PutP(1, 6, screen_buffer_ptr, map_data_buf(spriteIdx), frameDelay, yOffset)
            End If
        Next
        For j = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            If enemy_battle_data_ext(j) Then
                Call get_sprite_frame_data(6, j)
            End If
        Next
        Call PAL_WaitTime(5 + speed)
        Call update_shake()
    Next
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(5, player_hit_flags(0))
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(3, enemy_battle_data_ext(0))
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub produce_screen_map(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef viewportIdx)
    viewportX = x
    viewportY = y
    battle_sprite_data(0) = viewportX
    battle_sprite_data_ext(0) = viewportY
    Call PAL_ExRij(battle_sprite_data_ext(0), battle_sprite_data(0), save_temp_buf(0))
    For i = 0 To 4
        baseIdx = (i * 3) + 1
        dataIdx = baseIdx + 1
        dataIdx2 = baseIdx + 2
        battle_sprite_data(dataIdx) = viewportX
        battle_sprite_data_ext(dataIdx) = viewportY + (key_repeat_delay(viewportIdx) * 2)
        Call PAL_ExRij(battle_sprite_data_ext(dataIdx), battle_sprite_data(dataIdx), save_temp_buf(dataIdx))
        battle_sprite_data(dataIdx2) = viewportX + (fh_M_MSG_global(viewportIdx) * 2)
        battle_sprite_data_ext(dataIdx2) = viewportY
        Call PAL_ExRij(battle_sprite_data_ext(dataIdx2), battle_sprite_data(dataIdx2), save_temp_buf(dataIdx2))
        viewportX = viewportX + fh_M_MSG_global(viewportIdx)
        viewportY = viewportY + key_repeat_delay(viewportIdx)
        battle_sprite_data(baseIdx) = viewportX
        battle_sprite_data_ext(baseIdx) = viewportY
        Call PAL_ExRij(battle_sprite_data_ext(baseIdx), battle_sprite_data(baseIdx), save_temp_buf(baseIdx))
    Next
    screenX = -1
    For j = 0 To 12
        For baseIdx = 1 To curr_scene_event_count
            If npc_display_data(baseIdx).state > 0 Then
                If (((npc_display_data(baseIdx).triggerMode * 6) - 4) > j) And (npc_display_data(baseIdx).triggerMode <= 3) Then
                    mapX = npc_display_data(baseIdx).x
                    mapY = npc_display_data(baseIdx).y
                    Call PAL_ExRij(mapY, mapX, tileData)
                    If ((tileData = save_temp_buf(j)) And (mapX = battle_sprite_data(j))) And (mapY = battle_sprite_data_ext(j)) Then
                        screenX = baseIdx
                        GoTo L_0040FBF8
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next
    Next
L_0040FBF8:
    retval = screenX
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub render_dialog_control(ByRef x, ByRef y)
    image_draw_x = x
    image_draw_y = y
    image_lookup_key = 0
    For j = 1 To image_offset_table(0)
        k = image_offset_table(j)
        Select Case k
            Case 34
                Call swap_values(dialog_y, dialog_x)
            Case 36
                music_mode_init = image_offset_table(j + 1) - 48
                music_mode_init = music_mode_init * 10
                music_mode_init = music_mode_init + (image_offset_table(j + 2) - 48)
                music_mode_init = CSng (music_mode_init * 10) / CSng (7)
                j = j + 2
            Case 126
                tmp = image_offset_table(j + 1) - 48
                tmp = tmp * 10
                tmp = tmp + (image_offset_table(j + 2) - 48)
                tmp = CSng (tmp * 10) / CSng (7)
                flag_trigger = 0
                image_draw_flag = 0
                Call PAL_Delay(tmp)
                j = j + 2
            Case 41
                image_lookup_key = 1
            Case 40
                image_lookup_key = 2
        End Select
        If image_offset_table(j) > 128 Then
            dialogBuf(0) = image_offset_table(j)
            dialogBuf(1) = image_offset_table(j + 1)
            dialogBuf(2) = 0
            j = j + 1
            i = 16
        Else
            dialogBuf(0) = image_offset_table(j)
            dialogBuf(1) = 0
            i = 8
        End If
        Call PAL_DrawString(screen_buffer_ptr, dialog_x, 0, image_draw_y, image_draw_x, dialogBuf(0))
        If image_draw_flag = 0 Then
            Call PAL_WaitTime(music_mode_init)
        End If
        image_draw_x = image_draw_x + i
        tmp2 = check_key_pressed()
        If tmp2 = 2 Then
            image_draw_flag = -1
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub process_AutoScript(ByRef npcIdx, ByRef scriptEntry)
L_0040FF88:
    tmp = 0
    Call PAL_CopyMem(2, scriptEntry, tmp)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(8, sss_script_data_2(tmp * 8), save_temp_buf(0))
    opcode = save_temp_buf(0)
    operand1 = save_temp_buf(1)
    operand2 = save_temp_buf(2)
    operand3 = save_temp_buf(3)
    tmp3 = opcode
    If tmp3 = 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If tmp3 = 2 Then
        If operand2 = 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            Exit Sub
        End If
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto = npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto + 1
        If npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto < operand2 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            Exit Sub
        End If
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto = 0
        GoTo L_004102CE
    End If
    If tmp3 = 3 Then
        If operand2 = 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            GoTo L_0040FF88
        End If
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto = npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto + 1
        If npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto < operand2 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            GoTo L_0040FF88
        End If
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto = 0
        GoTo L_004102CE
    End If
    If tmp3 = 4 Then
        If operand2 > 0 Then
            tmp2 = operand2 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
            If (tmp2 > 0) And (tmp2 <= curr_scene_event_count) Then
                Call process_Script(operand1, tmp2)
            End If
            GoTo L_004102CE
        End If
        Call process_Script(operand1, npcIdx)
        GoTo L_004102CE
    End If
    If tmp3 = 6 Then
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        If CSng (operand1) < (rng * CSng (100)) Then
            If operand2 <> 0 Then
                scriptEntry = operand2
                GoTo L_0040FF88
            End If
            Exit Sub
        End If
        GoTo L_004102CE
    End If
    If tmp3 = 9 Then
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto = npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto + 1
        If npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto < operand1 Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).scriptIdleFrameAuto = 0
        GoTo L_004102CE
    End If
    If tmp3 > 10 Then
        Call process_scripts(operand3, operand2, operand1, opcode, scriptEntry, npcIdx)
    End If
L_004102CE:
    scriptEntry = increment_script_ip(scriptEntry)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub SaveRPG_internal(ByRef saveSlot)
    bstr = VB40032.rtcBstrFromAnsi(48 + saveSlot)
    filename = VB40032.rtcBstrFromAnsi(48 + saveSlot) & '.RPG'
    rpg_to_load = saveSlot
    result = open_file(-1, filename)
    max_save_number = max_save_number + 1
    save_temp_buf(0) = max_save_number
    save_temp_buf(1) = RPG_viewport_x
    save_temp_buf(2) = RPG_viewport_y
    save_temp_buf(3) = RPG_team_number
    save_temp_buf(4) = RPG_curr_scene
    save_temp_buf(5) = RPG_color_begin_ptr
    save_temp_buf(6) = RPG_team_direction
    save_temp_buf(7) = RPG_music_number
    save_temp_buf(8) = RPG_battle_music_number
    save_temp_buf(9) = RPG_battle_scene_number
    save_temp_buf(10) = RPG_screen_wave_grade
    save_temp_buf(11) = 0
    save_temp_buf(12) = RPG_current_calabash_number
    save_temp_buf(13) = RPG_role_locate_layer
    save_temp_buf(14) = RPG_ememy_chase_rate
    save_temp_buf(15) = RPG_change_chaserate_times
    save_temp_buf(16) = RPG_other_peoples
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, save_temp_buf(0), 40)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, RPG_money, 4)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, party(0).role, 50)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, trail(0).x, 30)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, playerExp(0, 0).exp, 384)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, playerRoles(0, 0), 900)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, poison_status(0, 0).poisonID, 320)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, inventory(0).item, 1536)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, scenes(1).mapNum, 2400)
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, objects(0).data0, 8400)
    Call load_event_objects()
    Call kernel32._hwrite(result, events(0), event_object_count * 32)
    Call kernel32._lclose(result)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub LoadRPG_internal(ByRef saveSlot)
    bstr = VB40032.rtcBstrFromAnsi(48 + saveSlot)
    filename = VB40032.rtcBstrFromAnsi(48 + saveSlot) & '.RPG'
    rpg_to_load = saveSlot
    result = open_file(0, filename)
    If result = 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    save_temp_buf(0) = 0
    Call kernel32._hread(result, save_temp_buf(0), 40)
    RPG_save_number = save_temp_buf(0)
    If RPG_save_number > 0 Then
        RPG_viewport_x = save_temp_buf(1)
        RPG_viewport_y = save_temp_buf(2)
        RPG_team_number = save_temp_buf(3)
        scene_to_load = save_temp_buf(4)
        RPG_curr_scene = scene_to_load
        RPG_color_begin_ptr = save_temp_buf(5)
        RPG_team_direction = save_temp_buf(6)
        RPG_music_number = save_temp_buf(7)
        RPG_battle_music_number = save_temp_buf(8)
        RPG_battle_scene_number = save_temp_buf(9)
        RPG_screen_wave_grade = save_temp_buf(10)
        RPG_current_calabash_number = save_temp_buf(12)
        RPG_role_locate_layer = save_temp_buf(13)
        RPG_ememy_chase_rate = save_temp_buf(14)
        RPG_change_chaserate_times = save_temp_buf(15)
        RPG_other_peoples = save_temp_buf(16)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, RPG_money, 4)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, party(0).role, 50)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, trail(0).x, 30)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, playerExp(0, 0).exp, 384)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, playerRoles(0, 0), 900)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, poison_status(0, 0).poisonID, 320)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, inventory(0).item, 1536)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, scenes(1).mapNum, 2400)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, objects(0).data0, 8400)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, events(0), event_object_count * 32)
        redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 7
        Call fade_in(1)
    End If
    Call kernel32._lclose(result)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub enemy_attack_enemy(ByRef attackerIdx, ByRef targetIdx)
    effect_particle_count = 0
    Call load_battle_effect_sprites()
    For i = 1 To 3
        enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).x = (enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).x + enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x) \ 2
        enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).y = (enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).y + enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y) \ 2
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Next
    effect_particle_count = 1
    effect_x_coords(1) = (enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).x + enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x) \ 2
    effect_y_coords(1) = (enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y - (enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).tileData \ 3)) + 10
    effect_frames(1) = 99
    stepSize = 16
    For i = 0 To 2
        enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x - stepSize
        stepSize = -stepSize \ 2
        effect_frame_count = 9 + i
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Next
    effect_particle_count = 0
    result = calc_sprite_y_offset(6, attackerIdx)
    result2 = calc_sprite_y_offset(4, targetIdx)
    damage = (calc_battle_damage(enemy_runtime_data(targetIdx).defense + result2, enemy_runtime_data(attackerIdx).attackStrength + result) * 2) \ enemy_runtime_data(targetIdx).physicalResistance
    Call add_damage_number(1, damage, enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y - 110, enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x)
    player_hit_flags(targetIdx) = -1
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).origX
    enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(attackerIdx).origY
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).hp = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).hp - damage
    If enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).hp > 0 Then
        enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).origX
        enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).origY
        Exit Sub
    End If
    npc_sprite_num = -1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub add_sprite_to_tree(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef depth, ByRef roleIdx, ByRef frameIdx, ByRef useNPC)
    If useNPC = 0 Then
        spriteBase = word_glyph_index(party(roleIdx).imageOffset + frameIdx) + party(roleIdx).imageOffset
        frameW = word_glyph_index(spriteBase)
        frameH = word_glyph_index(spriteBase + 1)
        Call PAL_AddToTree(word_glyph_index(spriteBase), depth + 6, (y + depth) + 10, x - (frameW \ 2))
    Else
        spriteBase = fire_mkf_data(npc_display_data(roleIdx).spritePtrOffset + frameIdx) + npc_display_data(roleIdx).spritePtrOffset
        frameW = fire_mkf_data(spriteBase)
        frameH = fire_mkf_data(spriteBase + 1)
        Call PAL_AddToTree(fire_mkf_data(spriteBase), depth + 2, (y + depth) + 9, x - (frameW \ 2))
    End If
    If multi_event_state <> 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If depth < 72 Then
        i = (frameH + 15) \ 16
        screenX = y + RPG_viewport_y
        j = frameW \ 64
        screenY = x + RPG_viewport_x
        tmp = screenX
        Call PAL_ExRij(tmp, screenY, k)
        For l = (tmp - i) To tmp
            For tileIdx = (screenY - j) To (screenY + j)
                Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l, tileIdx, k, screenX)
                Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l, tileIdx - 1, k, screenX)
                Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l, tileIdx + 1, k, screenX)
                If k = 0 Then
                    Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l, tileIdx, 1, screenX)
                    Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l, tileIdx - 1, 1, screenX)
                Else
                    Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l + 1, tileIdx + 1, 0, screenX)
                    Call PAL_ExBB(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0), l + 1, tileIdx, 0, screenX)
                End If
            Next
        Next
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub set_team_draw()
    If playerRoles(party(0).role, 64) = 4 Then
        party(0).frame = (RPG_team_direction * 4) + scene_flags
    Else
        party(0).frame = (RPG_team_direction * 3) + viewport_flags
    End If
    party(0).x = team_abstract_x
    party(0).y = team_abstract_y
    trail(0).direction = RPG_team_direction
    trail(0).x = x_off
    trail(0).y = y_off
    For i = 1 To RPG_team_number
        roleID = party(i).role
        trailIdx = 1
        trailX = trail(trailIdx).x - RPG_viewport_x
        trailY = trail(trailIdx).y - RPG_viewport_y
        If i = 2 Then
            trailY = trailY + 8
            If (trail(trailIdx).direction And 1) = 0 Then
                trailX = trailX + 16
                GoTo L_00411784
            End If
            trailX = trailX - 16
            GoTo L_00411784
        End If
        trailX = trailX - fh_M_MSG_global(trail(trailIdx).direction)
        trailY = trailY - key_repeat_delay(trail(trailIdx).direction)
L_00411784:
        If Not (load_map_data(0, RPG_viewport_y + trailY, RPG_viewport_x + trailX)) Then
            trailX = trail(trailIdx).x - RPG_viewport_x
            trailY = trail(trailIdx).y - RPG_viewport_y
        End If
        party(i).x = trailX
        party(i).y = trailY
        If playerRoles(roleID, 64) = 4 Then
            party(i).frame = (trail(2).direction * 4) + scene_flags
        Else
            party(i).frame = (trail(2).direction * 3) + viewport_flags_2
        End If
    Next
    For j = 1 To RPG_other_peoples
        i = RPG_team_number + j
        frameOffset = 2 + j
        party(i).x = trail(frameOffset).x - RPG_viewport_x
        party(i).y = trail(frameOffset).y - RPG_viewport_y
        party(i).frame = (trail(frameOffset).direction * 3) + viewport_flags_2
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub calc_display_exp(ByRef exp)
    Call frame_menu(-1, 8, 60, 76)
    Call draw_string(0, 3, word_dat_data(30), 70, 90)
    Call display_number(0, exp, 75, 200)
    Call wait_frame(310)
    For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        k = party(i).role
        If playerRoles(k, 9) > 0 Then
            playerExp(k, 0).level = playerRoles(k, 6)
            l = 0
            For j = 1 To 7
                l = l + playerExp(k, j).count
            Next
            If l <= 0 Then
                l = 1
            End If
            For j = 0 To 7
                If j > 0 Then
                    playerExp(k, j).exp = playerExp(k, j).exp + (((2 * exp) / CSng (l)) * CSng (playerExp(k, j).count))
                Else
                    playerExp(k, 0).exp = playerExp(k, 0).exp + exp
                End If
                Call PAL_CopyMem(200, data_levelup_exp(0), battle_order_array(0))
L_00411C02:
                If playerExp(k, j).exp >= battle_order_array(playerExp(k, j).level) Then
                    playerExp(k, j).exp = playerExp(k, j).exp - battle_order_array(playerExp(k, j).level)
                    playerExp(k, j).level = playerExp(k, j).level + 1
                    If playerExp(k, j).level > 99 Then
                        playerExp(k, j).level = 99
                        GoTo L_00411C02
                    End If
                    If j = 0 Then
                        Call show_equip_detail(1, k)
                        GoTo L_00411C02
                    End If
                    rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
                    Call increase_player_attr(CSng (1) + rng, j, k)
                    GoTo L_00411C02
                End If
            Next
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_status_icons()
    Call PAL_CopyMem(menu_bg_size, menu_bg_data(0), global_buf_2(0))
    If flag_battling Then
        iconY = 90
    Else
        iconY = 42
    End If
    For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
        player_battle_sprite(i).objectID = 0
        For j = 0 To 14
            For k = (j + 1) To 15
                If objects(poison_status(i, j).poisonID).data0 < objects(poison_status(i, k).poisonID).data0 Then
                    Call PAL_CopyMem(4, poison_status(i, j), l)
                    Call PAL_CopyMem(4, poison_status(i, k), poison_status(i, j))
                    Call PAL_CopyMem(4, l, poison_status(i, k))
                End If
            Next
        Next
        For j = 15 To 0 Step -1
            If poison_status(i, j).poisonID > 0 Then
                statusIcon = objects(poison_status(i, j).poisonID).data1
                If statusIcon > 0 Then
                    player_battle_sprite(i).objectID = statusIcon
                End If
            End If
        Next
        roleID = party(i).role
        If playerRoles(roleID, 9) <= 0 Then
            player_battle_sprite(i).objectID = 2
        End If
        Call PAL_PutP(0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(18)), 165, iconY)
        Call draw_player_status(player_battle_sprite(i).objectID, party(i).role, 160, iconY)
        If player_status(i, 3) > 0 Then
            Call draw_text_at(44, 26, 160, iconY + 50)
        End If
        If player_status(i, 2) > 0 Then
            Call draw_text_at(238, 28, 186, iconY + 24)
        End If
        If player_status(i, 1) > 0 Then
            Call draw_text_at(15, 27, 178, iconY + 42)
        End If
        If player_status(i, 0) > 0 Then
            Call draw_text_at(94, 29, 182, iconY + 60)
        End If
        iconY = iconY + 78
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_enemy_battle_frame()
    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
    tmp = bufPtr
    Call PAL_ExMyll(dialog_y_pos)
    For i = team_number To 0 Step -1
        If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
            spriteIdx = map_data_buf(enemy_battle_data(i).mapValue + enemy_battle_data(i).direction) + enemy_battle_data(i).mapValue
            y = enemy_battle_data(i).x - (map_data_buf(spriteIdx) \ 2)
            x = enemy_battle_data(i).y - map_data_buf(spriteIdx + 1)
            If player_hit_flags(i) Then
                Call PAL_PutP(1, 6, tmp, map_data_buf(spriteIdx), x, y)
            Else
                Call PAL_PutP(0, 0, tmp, map_data_buf(spriteIdx), x, y)
            End If
        End If
    Next
    If npc_dir_frame Then
        spriteIdx = mgo_frame_offsets(curr_npc_idx)
        y = npc_curr_frame - (mgo_frame_offsets(spriteIdx) \ 2)
        x = npc_direction - mgo_frame_offsets(spriteIdx + 1)
        Call PAL_PutP(0, 0, tmp, mgo_frame_offsets(spriteIdx), x, y)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If battle_select_max = 0 Then
        For j = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            save_temp_buf(j + 10) = player_battle_sprite(j).y
            save_temp_buf(j) = j
        Next
        For j = 0 To (enemy_pos_count - 1)
            For k = (j + 1) To enemy_pos_count
                If save_temp_buf(j + 10) > save_temp_buf(k + 10) Then
                    Call swap_values(save_temp_buf(k), save_temp_buf(j))
                    Call swap_values(save_temp_buf(k + 10), save_temp_buf(j + 10))
                End If
            Next
        Next
        For j = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            i = save_temp_buf(j)
            spriteIdx = mgo_frame_offsets(player_battle_sprite(i).spriteBase + player_battle_sprite(i).direction) + player_battle_sprite(i).spriteBase
            y = player_battle_sprite(i).x - (mgo_frame_offsets(spriteIdx) \ 2)
            x = player_battle_sprite(i).y - mgo_frame_offsets(spriteIdx + 1)
            Call PAL_PutP(0, 0, tmp, mgo_frame_offsets(spriteIdx), x, y)
        Next
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub buy_item_menu(ByRef storeID)
    retval = -1
    For i = 0 To 8
        itemID = data_store(i, storeID)
        If itemID > 0 Then
            retval = retval + 1
            battle_order_array(retval) = itemID
            battle_order_array(retval + 10) = objects(itemID).data1
        End If
    Next
    Call PAL_PutP(draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(-1, RPG_money, 141, 20, 21), draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(-1, 0, 100, 20, 35), 3, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(70)), 13, 46)
    Call PAL_PutP(0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(70)), 8, 40)
    menuX = 122
    Call draw_menu_table(-1, 9, 9, 9, 8, menuX)
    cursorPos = 0
    selection = -1
    While cursorPos <> -1
        menuY = 21
        For i = 0 To retval
            If i = cursorPos Then
                maxItems = 47
            Else
                maxItems = 78
            End If
            Call draw_text_at(maxItems, battle_order_array(i), menuY, menuX + 28)
            Call display_number(0, battle_order_array(i + 10), menuY + 5, menuX + 140)
            menuY = menuY + 18
        Next
        Call draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(0, RPG_money, 141, 20, 21)
        wordIdx = count_equipped_items(battle_order_array(cursorPos))
        invIdx = find_inventory_item(battle_order_array(cursorPos))
        If invIdx >= 0 Then
            wordIdx = wordIdx + inventory(invIdx).amount
        End If
        Call draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(0, wordIdx, 100, 20, 35)
        If cursorPos <> selection Then
            Call draw_object_icon(battle_order_array(cursorPos), 8, 40)
        End If
        selection = cursorPos
        keyResult = read_key()
        If keyResult = 2 Then
            If RPG_money >= battle_order_array(cursorPos + 10) Then
                Call push_screen_buffer()
                maxItems = yes_no_menu(19, confirmResult)
                If maxItems = 1 Then
                    RPG_money = RPG_money - battle_order_array(cursorPos + 10)
                    Call add_inventory_item(1, battle_order_array(cursorPos))
                End If
                Call restore_screen()
            End If
        End If
        If keyResult = 3 Then
            cursorPos = cursorPos - 1
        End If
        If keyResult = 4 Then
            cursorPos = cursorPos + 1
        End If
        If cursorPos < 0 Then
            cursorPos = 0
        End If
        If cursorPos > retval Then
            cursorPos = retval
        End If
        If keyResult = 1 Then
            cursorPos = -1
        End If
    Wend
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub level_up_player(ByRef roleID, ByRef levelCount, ByRef restoreHPMP)
    For i = 1 To levelCount
        playerRoles(roleID, 6) = playerRoles(roleID, 6) + 1
        playerExp(roleID, 0).level = playerRoles(roleID, 6)
        If playerRoles(roleID, 6) > 99 Then
            playerRoles(roleID, 6) = 99
        End If
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        playerRoles(roleID, 7) = CSng (playerRoles(roleID, 7) + 10) + Int (rng * CSng (8))
        If playerRoles(roleID, 7) > 999 Then
            playerRoles(roleID, 7) = 999
        End If
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        playerRoles(roleID, 8) = CSng (playerRoles(roleID, 8) + 8) + Int (rng * CSng (6))
        If playerRoles(roleID, 8) > 999 Then
            playerRoles(roleID, 8) = 999
        End If
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        playerRoles(roleID, 17) = CSng (playerRoles(roleID, 17) + 4) + rng
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        playerRoles(roleID, 18) = CSng (playerRoles(roleID, 18) + 4) + rng
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        playerRoles(roleID, 19) = CSng (playerRoles(roleID, 19) + 2) + rng
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        playerRoles(roleID, 20) = CSng (playerRoles(roleID, 20) + 2) + rng
        playerRoles(roleID, 21) = playerRoles(roleID, 21) + 2
        For j = 17 To 21
            If playerRoles(roleID, j) > 999 Then
                playerRoles(roleID, j) = 999
            End If
        Next
    Next
    If restoreHPMP Then
        playerRoles(roleID, 9) = playerRoles(roleID, 7)
        playerRoles(roleID, 10) = playerRoles(roleID, 8)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub player_attack_player(ByRef attackerIdx, ByRef targetIdx)
    retval = party(targetIdx).role
    result = get_player_attribute_total(19, retval)
    If player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = 3 Then
        result = result + result
    End If
    effect_particle_count = 0
    Call load_battle_effect_sprites()
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 7
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 3)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 0
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 7
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 8
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x + 32
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y + 10
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x - 4
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y + 2
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 9
    effect_particle_count = 1
    effect_frame_count = party(attackerIdx).role * 3
    effect_x_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x
    effect_y_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y - 10
    effect_frames(1) = 99
    k = 21
    For i = 0 To 2
        If i > 0 Then
            enemy_battle_data_ext(targetIdx) = -1
            If player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = 0 Then
                player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = 4
            End If
        End If
        player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x - k
        k = k \ 2
        player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y - k
        k = k \ 2
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
        effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
    Next
    effect_particle_count = 0
    result3 = get_player_attribute_total(17, party(attackerIdx).role)
    result2 = calc_battle_damage(result, result3)
    If result2 > playerRoles(retval, 9) Then
        result2 = playerRoles(retval, 9)
    End If
    playerRoles(retval, 9) = playerRoles(retval, 9) - result2
    Call add_damage_number(1, result2, player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y - 70, player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x)
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Call reset_battle_sprite_pos(attackerIdx)
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).origDirection
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_item_description(ByRef roleIdx, ByRef labelIdx, ByRef x, ByRef y)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(menu_bg_size, menu_bg_data(0), global_buf_2(0))
    If (roleIdx < 0) Or (roleIdx > RPG_team_number) Then
        roleIdx = 0
    End If
    itemID = inventory(y).item
    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
    screen_buffer_ptr = bufPtr
    Call PAL_PushScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    i = 0
    Call PAL_PutP(draw_menu_table(i, 8, 10, 0, x, labelIdx), 3, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(70)), x + 84, labelIdx + 14)
    Call PAL_PutP(0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(70)), x + 79, labelIdx + 8)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0))
    i = -1
L_004135B4:
    Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0))
    Call show_role_attributes(-1, party(roleIdx).role, x + 12, labelIdx + 84)
    menuY = labelIdx + 14
    menuX = x + 12
    For j = 0 To RPG_team_number
        If roleIdx = j Then
            itemX = 250
        Else
            itemX = 78
        End If
        Call draw_text_at(itemX, playerRoles(party(j).role, 3), menuX, menuY)
        menuX = menuX + 22
    Next
    amount = inventory(y).amount
    If amount > 0 Then
        Call PAL_PutP(read_ball_mkf_index(objects(itemID).data0), 0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(0)), x + 86, labelIdx + 16)
        Call draw_text_at(13, itemID, x + 142, labelIdx + 4)
    End If
    If amount > 1 Then
        Call display_number(2, amount, x + 132, labelIdx + 62)
    End If
    Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    If amount <= 0 Then
        Call wait_frame(150)
        GoTo L_00413902
    End If
    itemX = read_key()
    cursorPos = itemX
    If cursorPos = 3 Then
        roleIdx = roleIdx - 1
        If roleIdx < 0 Then
            roleIdx = RPG_team_number
        End If
        GoTo L_004135B4
    End If
    If cursorPos = 4 Then
        roleIdx = roleIdx + 1
        If roleIdx > RPG_team_number Then
            roleIdx = 0
        End If
        GoTo L_004135B4
    End If
    If cursorPos > 4 Then
        GoTo L_004135B4
    End If
    If cursorPos = 2 Then
        Call process_Script(objects(itemID).data2, roleIdx)
        If redraw_hp_mp_flag Then
            Call PAL_GetBin(3, objects(itemID).data6, descIdx)
            If descIdx Then
                Call remove_inventory_item(1, itemID)
            End If
        End If
        GoTo L_004135B4
    End If
    If cursorPos = 1 Then
        Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(64000))
        roleIdx = -1
    End If
L_00413902:
    screen_buffer_ptr = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub process_event_objects(ByRef checkTrigger)
    If palette_fade_active <> 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    For i = 1 To curr_scene_event_count
        If npc_display_data(i).state <> 0 Then
            npc_display_data(i).vanishTime = npc_display_data(i).vanishTime - Sgn (npc_display_data(i).vanishTime)
            If npc_display_data(i).state > 0 Then
                If (checkTrigger And (npc_display_data(i).vanishTime = 0)) And (npc_display_data(i).triggerMode >= 4) Then
                    npcX = ((npc_display_data(i).triggerMode - 4) * 32) + 16
                    npcY = party_abs_x - npc_display_data(i).x
                    partyX = party_abs_y - npc_display_data(i).y
                    If (Abs (npcY) + (Abs (partyX) * 2)) < npcX Then
                        If npc_display_data(i).spriteFrames > 0 Then
                            npc_display_data(i).currentFrame = 0
                            Call PAL_ExTF(init_walk_frames(), partyX, npcY, npc_display_data(i).direction)
                            Call check_in_battle(1)
                        End If
                        scene_enter_flag = 0
                        scene_leave_flag = 0
                        Call process_Script(npc_display_data(i).triggerScript, i)
                    End If
                End If
                GoTo L_00413BCE
            End If
            If npc_display_data(i).vanishTime = 0 Then
                partyY = npc_display_data(i).x - RPG_viewport_x
                dx = npc_display_data(i).y - RPG_viewport_y
                If (((partyY < 0) Or (partyY > 320)) Or (dx < 0)) Or (dx > 220) Then
                    npc_display_data(i).state = Abs (npc_display_data(i).state)
                    npc_display_data(i).currentFrame = 0
                End If
            End If
        End If
L_00413BCE:
        If scene_to_load <> RPG_curr_scene Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next
    For i = 1 To curr_scene_event_count
        If npc_display_data(i).state > 0 Then
            If npc_display_data(i).vanishTime = 0 Then
                Call process_AutoScript(npc_display_data(i).autoScript, i)
            End If
            If (checkTrigger And (npc_display_data(i).state = 2)) And (npc_display_data(i).spriteNum > 0) Then
                npcY = party_abs_x - npc_display_data(i).x
                partyX = party_abs_y - npc_display_data(i).y
                If (Abs (npcY) + (Abs (partyX) * 2)) < 13 Then
                    triggerScript = npc_display_data(i).direction
                    Do
                        triggerScript = (triggerScript + 1) And 3
                        partyY = party_abs_x + fh_M_MSG_global(triggerScript)
                        dx = party_abs_y + key_repeat_delay(triggerScript)
                        If load_map_data(0, dx, partyY) Then
                            x_off = party_abs_x
                            y_off = party_abs_y
                            party_abs_x = partyY
                            party_abs_y = dx
                            viewport_x_bak = RPG_viewport_x
                            viewport_y_bak = RPG_viewport_y
                            RPG_viewport_x = party_abs_x - team_abstract_x
                            RPG_viewport_y = party_abs_y - team_abstract_y
                            Call update_viewport_scroll()
                        End If
                    Loop While triggerScript <> npc_display_data(i).direction
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next
    RPG_change_chaserate_times = RPG_change_chaserate_times - 1
    If RPG_change_chaserate_times <= 0 Then
        RPG_ememy_chase_rate = 1
        RPG_change_chaserate_times = 0
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub Load_system_files()
    filename = 'SSS.MKF'
    fh_temp = open_file(0, filename)
    Call kernel32._hread(fh_temp, file_offset_table(0), 24)
    event_object_count = tmp_file_size / CSng (32)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(events, 0), read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 1), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), scenes(1).mapNum)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 2), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), objects(0).data0)
    Call read_mkf_subfile(sss_script_data, 3)
    Call read_mkf_subfile(sss_script_data_2, 4)
    sss_subfile_count = (tmp_file_size / CSng (8)) - CSng (1)
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_temp)
    filename = 'DATA.MKF'
    fh_temp = open_file(0, filename)
    Call kernel32._hread(fh_temp, file_offset_table(0), 64)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 0), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), data_store(0, 0))
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(data_enemy, 1), read_mkf_subfile(data_enemy_team, 2), read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 3), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), playerRoles(0, 0))
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(data_magic, 4), read_mkf_subfile(data_battlefield, 5), read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 6), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), data_levelup_magic(0, 0).level)
    menu_bg_size = tmp_file_size
    data_mkf_handle = tmp_file_size
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(menu_bg_data, 9), read_mkf_subfile(data_object_ext, 10), read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 11), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), battle_effect_data(0).effect)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 12), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), data_mkf_chunk12(0))
    Call PAL_CopyMem(read_mkf_subfile(global_buf_1, 13), tmp_file_size, global_buf_1(0), enemy_pos_data(0, 0).x)
    Call read_mkf_subfile(data_levelup_exp, 14)
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_temp)
    filename = 'BALL.MKF'
    fh_temp = open_file(0, filename)
    filePos = kernel32._llseek(fh_temp, 0, 2)
    tmp = filePos
    filePos = kernel32._llseek(fh_temp, 0, 0)
    tmp2 = filePos
    Call kernel32._hread(fh_temp, ball_mkf_data(0), tmp)
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_temp)
    Open 'WORD.DAT', 2, 10
    For i = 0 To 564
        Get 2, word_dat_data(i)
    Next
    Close 2
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub player_attack_all(ByRef roleIdx, ByRef animate)
    roleID = party(roleIdx).role
    hitCount = 69
    l = CSng (1)
    If (player_status(roleIdx, 5) > 0) Or (Int (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (6)) = CSng (4)) Then
        l = CSng (3)
        hitCount = 71
    End If
    hitCount = playerRoles(roleID, hitCount)
    effect_particle_count = 0
    Call load_battle_effect_sprites()
    If animate = 0 Then
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 7
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 4)
    End If
    Call play_sound(1, hitCount)
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 8
    If animate = 0 Then
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x - 40
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y - 18
    End If
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x - 10
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y - 5
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 9
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x - 2
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y - 1
    effect_particle_count = 0
    effect_frame_count = battle_effect_data(player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).spriteNum).frames * 3
    For i = 0 To team_number
        If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
            effect_particle_count = effect_particle_count + 1
            effect_x_coords(effect_particle_count) = enemy_battle_data(i).x
            effect_y_coords(effect_particle_count) = (enemy_battle_data(i).y - (enemy_battle_data(i).tileData \ 3)) + 10
            effect_frames(effect_particle_count) = 99
        End If
    Next
    Call play_sound(1, playerRoles(roleID, 70))
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
    For j = 0 To 4
        i = key_direction_map(j)
        If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
            battle_order_array(i) = -1
            targetRole = get_player_attribute_total(17, roleID)
            tmp = calc_sprite_y_offset(4, i)
            targetIdx = (calc_battle_damage(enemy_runtime_data(i).defense + tmp, targetRole) * 2) \ enemy_runtime_data(i).physicalResistance
            targetIdx = CSng (targetIdx) * l
            battle_order_array(i + 20) = battle_order_array(i + 20) + targetIdx
            l = l / CSng (2)
            Call add_damage_number(1, targetIdx, enemy_battle_data(i).y - 110, enemy_battle_data(i).x)
            player_hit_flags(i) = -1
        End If
    Next
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x + 2
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y + 1
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    effect_particle_count = 0
    Call enemy_hit_reaction(3)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_viewport_scroll()
    viewport_scroll_x = relative_viewport_x
    viewport_scroll_y = relative_viewport_y
    relative_viewport_x = (((relative_viewport_x + RPG_viewport_x) - viewport_x_bak) + 320) Mod 320
    relative_viewport_y = (((relative_viewport_y + RPG_viewport_y) - viewport_y_bak) + 200) Mod 200
    If multi_event_state <> 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If (Abs (RPG_viewport_x - viewport_x_bak) >= 320) Or (Abs (RPG_viewport_y - viewport_y_bak) >= 200) Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    scrollDirX = 0
    If RPG_viewport_x <> viewport_x_bak Then
        scrollDirX = 1
        If RPG_viewport_x > viewport_x_bak Then
            srcX = viewport_x_bak + 320
            If relative_viewport_x = 0 Then
                srcTileX1 = viewport_scroll_x
                srcTileX2 = 319
                GoTo L_00414B82
            End If
            If relative_viewport_x > viewport_scroll_x Then
                srcTileX1 = viewport_scroll_x
                srcTileX2 = relative_viewport_x - 1
                GoTo L_00414B82
            End If
            scrollDirX = 2
            srcTileX1 = viewport_scroll_x
            srcTileX2 = 319
            srcX = viewport_x_bak + 320
            srcTileX1b = 0
            srcTileX2b = relative_viewport_x - 1
            tmp3 = srcX + (320 - srcTileX1)
            GoTo L_00414B82
        End If
        srcX = RPG_viewport_x
        If viewport_scroll_x = 0 Then
            srcTileX1 = relative_viewport_x
            srcTileX2 = 319
            GoTo L_00414B82
        End If
        If relative_viewport_x > viewport_scroll_x Then
            scrollDirX = 2
            srcTileX1 = relative_viewport_x
            srcTileX2 = 319
            srcTileX1b = 0
            srcTileX2b = viewport_scroll_x - 1
            tmp3 = srcX + (320 - srcTileX1)
            GoTo L_00414B82
        End If
        srcTileX1 = relative_viewport_x
        srcTileX2 = viewport_scroll_x - 1
    End If
L_00414B82:
    scrollDirY = 0
    If relative_viewport_y <> viewport_scroll_y Then
        scrollDirY = 1
        If RPG_viewport_y > viewport_y_bak Then
            srcY = viewport_y_bak + 200
            If relative_viewport_y = 0 Then
                srcTileY1 = viewport_scroll_y
                srcTileY2 = 199
                GoTo L_00414CF4
            End If
            If relative_viewport_y > viewport_scroll_y Then
                srcTileY1 = viewport_scroll_y
                srcTileY2 = relative_viewport_y - 1
                GoTo L_00414CF4
            End If
            scrollDirY = 2
            srcTileY1 = viewport_scroll_y
            srcTileY2 = 199
            srcTileY1b = 0
            srcTileY2b = relative_viewport_y - 1
            tmp = srcY + (200 - srcTileY1)
            GoTo L_00414CF4
        End If
        srcY = RPG_viewport_y
        If viewport_scroll_y = 0 Then
            srcTileY1 = relative_viewport_y
            srcTileY2 = 199
            GoTo L_00414CF4
        End If
        If relative_viewport_y > viewport_scroll_y Then
            scrollDirY = 2
            srcTileY1 = relative_viewport_y
            srcTileY2 = 199
            srcTileY1b = 0
            srcTileY2b = viewport_scroll_y - 1
            tmp = srcY + (200 - srcTileY1)
            GoTo L_00414CF4
        End If
        srcTileY1 = relative_viewport_y
        srcTileY2 = viewport_scroll_y - 1
    End If
L_00414CF4:
    dstTileY = RPG_viewport_y + (200 - relative_viewport_y)
    dstTileX = RPG_viewport_x + (320 - relative_viewport_x)
    If scrollDirX > 0 Then
        If relative_viewport_y > 0 Then
            Call redraw_tile(dstTileY, srcX, relative_viewport_y - 1, srcTileX2, 0, srcTileX1)
        End If
        Call redraw_tile(RPG_viewport_y, srcX, 199, srcTileX2, relative_viewport_y, srcTileX1)
    End If
    If scrollDirX = 2 Then
        If relative_viewport_y > 0 Then
            Call redraw_tile(dstTileY, tmp3, relative_viewport_y - 1, srcTileX2b, 0, srcTileX1b)
        End If
        Call redraw_tile(RPG_viewport_y, tmp3, 199, srcTileX2b, relative_viewport_y, srcTileX1b)
    End If
    If scrollDirY > 0 Then
        If relative_viewport_x > 0 Then
            Call redraw_tile(srcY, dstTileX, srcTileY2, relative_viewport_x - 1, srcTileY1, 0)
        End If
        Call redraw_tile(srcY, RPG_viewport_x, srcTileY2, 319, srcTileY1, relative_viewport_x)
    End If
    If scrollDirY = 2 Then
        If relative_viewport_x > 0 Then
            Call redraw_tile(tmp, dstTileX, srcTileY2b, relative_viewport_x - 1, srcTileY1b, 0)
        End If
        Call redraw_tile(tmp, RPG_viewport_x, srcTileY2b, 319, srcTileY1b, relative_viewport_x)
    End If
    Call PAL_VWindow(199, 319, 0, 0)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub enemy_attack_role(ByRef enemyIdx, targetRole, itemID)
    in_battle_action = -1
    If enemy_status(enemyIdx, 0) <= 0 Then
        Call process_Script(enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).battleEndScript, enemyIdx)
        i = 0
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        If CSng (enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicRate) >= (rng * CSng (10)) Then
            magicIdx = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magic
        Else
            magicIdx = 0
        End If
        If (magicIdx > 0) And enemy_status(enemyIdx, 3) Then
            magicIdx = 0
            i = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        End If
        If (magicIdx < 0) Or i Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If magicIdx = 0 Then
            attackerDmg = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackStrength + calc_sprite_y_offset(6, enemyIdx)
            Call enemy_physical_attack(itemID, attackerDmg, targetRole, enemyIdx)
        Else
            itemObj = objects(magicIdx).data0
            attackerDmg = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicStrength + calc_sprite_y_offset(6, enemyIdx)
            Call enemy_magical_attack(attackerDmg, magicIdx, targetRole, enemyIdx)
            Call process_Script(objects(magicIdx).data2, targetRole)
        End If
        targetFound = -1
        For j = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            itemObj = party(j).role
            If (player_battle_sprite(j).origDirection = 0) And (playerRoles(itemObj, 9) > 0) Then
                If (playerRoles(itemObj, 9) < 50) And (playerRoles(itemObj, 9) <= (playerRoles(itemObj, 7) \ 5)) Then
                    Call play_sound(1, playerRoles(itemObj, 74))
                End If
                If playerRoles(itemObj, 9) < 10 Then
                    If VB40032.rtcRandomNext() And (playerRoles(playerRoles(itemObj, 31), 9) > 0) Then
                        targetFound = j
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next
        Call update_player_battle_status()
        targetSelected = -1
        k = 10
        For j = 1 To 5
            For l = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                If battle_role_action(l).flag = -1 Then
                    player_battle_sprite(l).x = player_battle_sprite(l).x + k
                    player_battle_sprite(l).y = player_battle_sprite(l).y + (k \ 2)
                    For m = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                        If VB40032.rtcRandomNext() And (party(m).role = playerRoles(party(l).role, 31)) Then
                            targetSelected = m
                        End If
                    Next
                End If
            Next
            Call draw_battle_scene(-1, 1)
            k = k \ 2
        Next
        If targetSelected >= 0 Then
            If playerRoles(party(targetSelected).role, 9) > 0 Then
                Call process_Script(objects(playerRoles(party(targetSelected).role, 3)).data2, targetSelected)
                GoTo L_004154DC
            End If
            If targetFound >= 0 Then
                Call process_Script(objects(playerRoles(party(targetFound).role, 3)).data3, targetFound)
            End If
            GoTo L_004154DC
        End If
        If targetFound >= 0 Then
            Call process_Script(objects(playerRoles(party(targetFound).role, 3)).data3, targetFound)
        End If
        GoTo L_004154DC
    End If
    k = random_enemy_id()
    If k <> enemyIdx Then
        Call enemy_attack_enemy(k, enemyIdx)
    End If
L_004154DC:
    in_battle_action = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub role_physical_attack(ByRef attackerIdx, ByRef targetIdx, ByRef targetRole, ByRef isCritical)
    attackerRole = party(attackerIdx).role
    effect_particle_count = 0
    Call load_battle_effect_sprites()
    If targetIdx > 2 Then
        damage = (targetIdx - attackerIdx) * 8
    End If
    If isCritical = 0 Then
        player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 7
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 4)
    End If
    rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
    targetRole = CSng (targetRole + 1) + rng
    If (Int (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (6)) = CSng (3)) Or (player_status(attackerIdx, 5) > 0) Then
        targetRole = targetRole * 3
        hitCount = 2
        targetHP = playerRoles(attackerRole, 71)
    Else
        hitCount = 1
        targetHP = playerRoles(attackerRole, 69)
    End If
    rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
    targetRole = CSng (targetRole) * (CSng (1) + (rng / CSng (8)))
    If (attackerRole = 0) And (Int (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (12)) = CSng (3)) Then
        targetRole = targetRole * 2
        hitCount = 2
        targetHP = playerRoles(attackerRole, 71)
    End If
    Call play_sound(1, targetHP)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 8
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x = (enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x + 64) - damage
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y = (enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y + 20) + damage
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).animOffset = 4
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x - 10
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y - 2
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).direction = 9
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x - 16
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y - 4
    effect_particle_count = hitCount
    effect_frame_count = battle_effect_data(player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).spriteNum).frames * 3
    targetX = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x
    targetY = (enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y - (enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).tileData \ 3)) + 10
    For j = 1 To effect_particle_count
        effect_x_coords(j) = targetX
        effect_y_coords(j) = targetY
        effect_frames(j) = 50
        targetX = targetX - 16
        targetY = targetY + 16
    Next
    Call play_sound(1, playerRoles(attackerRole, 70))
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
    Call add_damage_number(1, targetRole, enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y - 110, enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x)
    player_hit_flags(targetIdx) = -1
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).x + 2
    player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(attackerIdx).y + 1
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    effect_particle_count = 0
    frameDelay = 8
    For j = 0 To 2
        enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).x - frameDelay
        frameDelay = frameDelay \ 2
        enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).y - frameDelay
        frameDelay = -frameDelay
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub inventory_use_menu()
L_00415B1C:
    itemObj = select_item_with_filter(2, 0, magic_select_tmp)
    If itemObj < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(load_fbp_subfile(1), global_buf_1(0))
    screen_buffer_ptr = bufPtr
    Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0))
    j = 0
L_00415BA6:
    itemType = objects(itemObj).data6 \ 64
    For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
        battle_order_array(i) = playerRoles(party(i).role, 3)
        Call PAL_GetBin(party(i).role, itemType, battle_order_array(100 + i))
    Next
L_00415C46:
    Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0))
    Call PAL_PutP(read_ball_mkf_index(objects(itemObj).data0), 0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(0)), 15, 16)
    Call draw_text_at(13, itemObj, 69, 4)
    If find_inventory_item(itemObj) >= 0 Then
        invIdx = find_inventory_item(itemObj)
        Call display_number(2, inventory(invIdx).amount, 72, 72)
    End If
    roleID = party(j).role
    menuX = 110
    Call draw_menu_table(-1, RPG_team_number + 1, 3, 0, menuX - 12, 3)
    For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
        If battle_order_array(i + 100) Then
            If j = i Then
                menuY = 250
                GoTo L_00415E00
            End If
            menuY = 78
            GoTo L_00415E00
        End If
        If j = i Then
            menuY = 28
            GoTo L_00415E00
        End If
        menuY = 24
L_00415E00:
        Call draw_text_at(menuY, playerRoles(party(i).role, 3), menuX, 16)
        menuX = menuX + 18
    Next
    menuX = 11
    For equipSlot = 11 To 16
        Call draw_text_at(78, playerRoles(roleID, equipSlot), menuX, 132)
        menuX = menuX + 22
    Next
    menuX = 16
    For equipSlot = 17 To 21
        currEquip = playerRoles(roleID, equipSlot)
        For k = 11 To 16
            currEquip = currEquip + equipment_effect(roleID, k, equipSlot)
        Next
        Call display_number(0, currEquip, menuX, 278)
        menuX = menuX + 22
    Next
    Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    k = read_key()
    If itemObj > 0 Then
        m = k
        If m = 3 Then
            j = j - 1
            If j < 0 Then
                j = RPG_team_number
            End If
            GoTo L_00415C46
        End If
        If m = 4 Then
            j = j + 1
            If j > RPG_team_number Then
                j = 0
            End If
            GoTo L_00415C46
        End If
        If m = 2 Then
            If battle_order_array(j + 100) Then
                maxHP = playerRoles(l, 65)
                Call process_Script(objects(itemObj).data3, j)
                playerRoles(l, 65) = maxHP
                Call remove_inventory_item(1, itemObj)
                Call add_inventory_item(1, coop_magic_tmp)
                itemObj = coop_magic_tmp
                If itemObj > 0 Then
                    GoTo L_00415BA6
                End If
                GoTo L_00415C46
            End If
            GoTo L_00415C46
        End If
        If m > 4 Then
            GoTo L_00415C46
        End If
        If m = 1 Then
            GoTo L_00415B1C
        End If
    End If
L_004160F6:
    invResult = compact_inventory()
    screen_buffer_ptr = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_role_status(ByRef roleIdx)
    roleID = party(roleIdx).role
    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(load_fbp_subfile(0), global_buf_1(0))
    screen_buffer_ptr = bufPtr
    Call PAL_CopyMem(draw_text_at(78, 2, 8, 6), display_number(0, playerExp(roleID, 0).exp, 8, 66), 200, data_levelup_exp(0), battle_order_array(0))
    Call display_number(1, battle_order_array(playerExp(roleID, 0).level), 18, 66)
    Call draw_text_at(78, 48, 34, 6)
    Call display_number(0, playerRoles(roleID, 6), 38, 64)
    menuX = 58
    For j = 7 To 8
        Call PAL_PutP(draw_text_at(78, 42 + j, menuX - 1, 6), display_number(0, playerRoles(roleID, j + 2), menuX, 70), 0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(39)), menuX + 3, 74)
        Call display_number(1, playerRoles(roleID, j), menuX + 6, 90)
        menuX = menuX + 22
    Next
    For j = 17 To 21
        Call draw_text_at(78, j + 34, menuX - 4, 6)
        tmp = get_player_attribute_total(j, roleID)
        Call display_number(0, tmp, menuX, 70)
        menuX = menuX + 20
    Next
    battle_sprite_data(0) = 190
    battle_sprite_data_ext(0) = 0
    battle_sprite_data(1) = 248
    battle_sprite_data_ext(1) = 40
    battle_sprite_data(2) = 252
    battle_sprite_data_ext(2) = 102
    battle_sprite_data(3) = 202
    battle_sprite_data_ext(3) = 134
    battle_sprite_data(4) = 142
    battle_sprite_data_ext(4) = 142
    battle_sprite_data(5) = 82
    battle_sprite_data_ext(5) = 126
    For j = 0 To 5
        attrVal = playerRoles(roleID, j + 11)
        If attrVal > 0 Then
            Call PAL_PutP(read_ball_mkf_index(objects(attrVal).data0), 0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(0)), battle_sprite_data_ext(j), battle_sprite_data(j))
            Call draw_text_at(189, attrVal, battle_sprite_data_ext(j) + 42, battle_sprite_data(j) + 4)
        End If
    Next
    Call show_face(playerRoles(roleID, 0), 78, 155)
    Call draw_text_at(78, playerRoles(roleID, 3), 8, 110)
    attrVal = 0
    For i = 0 To 15
        equipObjID = poison_status(roleIdx, i).poisonID
        equipAttr = objects(equipObjID).data1
        If ((equipAttr > 0) And (equipObjID > 0)) And (attrVal < 4) Then
            Call draw_text_at(equipAttr + 10, equipObjID, 60 + (attrVal * 18), 188)
            attrVal = attrVal + 1
        End If
    Next
    Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    screen_buffer_ptr = 0
    If flag_battling Then
        Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0))
    End If
    keyResult = read_key()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_thurgy(ByRef roleID, ByRef x, ByRef y)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(sort_player_magic(roleID), 32, battle_order_array(0))
    maxMagic = -1
    For i = 32 To 63
        j = i - 32
        magicObjID = playerRoles(roleID, i)
        If magicObjID > 0 Then
            battle_order_array(j) = 1
            maxMagic = maxMagic + 1
            If objects(magicObjID).data6 And y Then
                Call copy_subfile_data(objects(playerRoles(roleID, i)).data0)
                If playerRoles(roleID, 10) >= theurgy_data(0).costMP Then
                    battle_order_array(j) = 2
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next
    menuX = 10
    menuY = 40
    colCount = 3
    rowCount = 5
    maxPage = 2
    k = colCount - 1
    tmp = rowCount - 1
    maxScroll = ((maxMagic + k) \ colCount) - maxPage
    If maxScroll < 0 Then
        maxScroll = 0
    End If
    If x > maxMagic Then
        x = maxMagic
    End If
    rowPos = x \ 3
    colPos = x Mod 3
    If rowPos > maxPage Then
        l = rowPos - maxPage
        rowPos = maxPage
    End If
    If y = 1 Then
        p = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
        screen_buffer_ptr = p
        Call PAL_PushScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    Else
        p = PAL_ArrayPtr(fire_mkf_data(0))
        screen_buffer_ptr = p
        Call PAL_PushScreen(fire_mkf_data(0))
    End If
    prevCol = 0
    prevRow = 0
    prevScroll = 0
    inputResult = -2
    While inputResult = -2
        Call draw_menu_table(0, rowCount, 17, 9, menuY, menuX)
        If l < 0 Then
            l = 0
        End If
        If l > maxScroll Then
            l = maxScroll
        End If
        If l > 0 Then
            If rowPos < maxPage Then
                rowPos = maxPage
                l = l - 1
            End If
        End If
        If l < maxScroll Then
            If rowPos > maxPage Then
                rowPos = maxPage
                l = l + 1
            End If
        End If
        Call PAL_Ffxy(tmp, k, rowPos, colPos)
        x = ((l + rowPos) * colCount) + colPos
        If battle_order_array(x) = 0 Then
            colPos = prevCol
            rowPos = prevRow
            l = prevScroll
            x = ((l + rowPos) * colCount) + colPos
            If x > maxMagic Then
                colPos = 0
                rowPos = 0
                x = 0
                l = 0
            End If
        End If
        currMagicObj = playerRoles(roleID, 32 + x)
        Call PAL_PutP(copy_subfile_data(objects(currMagicObj).data0), frame_menu(0, 5, 0, 0), display_number(0, theurgy_data(0).costMP, 13, 20), 0, 0, screen_buffer_ptr, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(39)), 13, 28)
        Call display_number(2, playerRoles(roleID, 10), 13, 46)
        m = menuY + 12
        tmp3 = l * colCount
        For n = 0 To tmp
            tmp2 = menuX + 24
            For tmp4 = 0 To k
                If battle_order_array(tmp3) > 0 Then
                    If battle_order_array(tmp3) = 2 Then
                        If (tmp3 = x) And (inputResult = -2) Then
                            highlightColor = 250
                            GoTo L_00416D32
                        End If
                        highlightColor = 78
                        GoTo L_00416D32
                    End If
                    If (tmp3 = x) And (inputResult = -2) Then
                        highlightColor = 28
                        GoTo L_00416D32
                    End If
                    highlightColor = 24
L_00416D32:
                    Call draw_text_at(highlightColor, playerRoles(roleID, tmp3 + 32), m, tmp2)
                    If tmp3 = x Then
                        Call make_dialog_frame(m + 11, tmp2 + 24)
                    End If
                End If
                tmp2 = tmp2 + 88
                tmp3 = tmp3 + 1
            Next
            m = m + 18
        Next
        If y = 1 Then
            Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
        Else
            Call PAL_PopScreen(fire_mkf_data(0))
        End If
        savedScreen = screen_buffer_ptr
        screen_buffer_ptr = 0
        Call process_Script(objects(currMagicObj).data5, 2)
        screen_buffer_ptr = savedScreen
        j = read_key()
        prevCol = colPos
        prevRow = rowPos
        prevScroll = l
        If j = 3 Then
            rowPos = rowPos - 1
        End If
        If j = 4 Then
            rowPos = rowPos + 1
        End If
        If j = 5 Then
            colPos = colPos - 1
        End If
        If j = 6 Then
            colPos = colPos + 1
        End If
        If j = 7 Then
            l = l - maxPage
        End If
        If j = 8 Then
            l = l + maxPage
        End If
        If j = 1 Then
            inputResult = -1
        End If
        If (j = 2) And (battle_order_array(x) = 2) Then
            inputResult = x
        End If
    Wend
    screen_buffer_ptr = 0
    If inputResult >= 0 Then
        inputResult = playerRoles(roleID, inputResult + 32)
    End If
    magicCount = inputResult
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub process_Script(ByRef eventObjID, ByRef scriptEntry)
    redraw_hp_mp_flag = -1
    continueFlag = 0
    savedEntry = scriptEntry
    flag_trigger = 0
    flag_key_updown = 0
    dialog_x = 79
    dialog_y = 45
    dialog_color = 26
    menu_cursor_pos = 141
    dialog_type = 1
    dialog_text_x = 12
    dialog_text_y = 8
    dialog_width = 44
    dialog_height = 26
L_004178B2:
    Call PAL_CvLong(subfileIdx, scriptEntry)
    If (subfileIdx > sss_subfile_count) Or (scriptEntry = 0) Then
        GoTo L_00418098
    End If
    Call PAL_CopyMem(8, sss_script_data_2(subfileIdx * 8), save_temp_buf(0))
    opcode = save_temp_buf(0)
    operand1 = save_temp_buf(1)
    operand2 = save_temp_buf(2)
    operand3 = save_temp_buf(3)
    tmp2 = opcode
    If tmp2 = -1 Then
        If flag_trigger > 3 Then
            Call show_dialog_image_and_wait()
            flag_trigger = 0
            Call restore_screen()
        End If
        If flag_trigger = 0 Then
            dialogY = dialog_width
            dialogH = dialog_height
            If mutex_can_change_palette = 0 Then
                Call push_screen_buffer()
            End If
        End If
        If dialog_type >= 10 Then
            imgOffset = image_offset_table(0) \ 2
            dialogY = dialog_width - (imgOffset * 8)
            Call PAL_DrawString(load_script_data(operand1), frame_menu(0, imgOffset, dialogH, dialogY), screen_buffer_ptr, 64, 3, dialogH + 8, dialogY + 6, image_offset_table(1))
            dialogH = dialogH + 18
            flag_key_updown = flag_key_updown + 1
            flag_trigger = flag_key_updown
            GoTo L_0041805E
        End If
        Call load_script_data(operand1)
        imgOffset = image_offset_table(0)
        If ((dialog_type > 0) And (flag_trigger = 0)) And ((((image_offset_table(imgOffset - 1) = 161) And (image_offset_table(imgOffset) = 71)) Or ((image_offset_table(imgOffset - 1) = 163) And (image_offset_table(imgOffset) = 186))) Or (image_offset_table(imgOffset) = 58)) Then
            Call PAL_DrawString(screen_buffer_ptr, 140, 0, dialog_text_y, dialog_text_x, image_offset_table(1))
            GoTo L_0041805E
        End If
        If dialog_type = 9 Then
            If dialogH > 100 Then
                dialogTemp = 0
            Else
                dialogTemp = 28
            End If
            Call PAL_DrawString(screen_buffer_ptr, dialog_x, 0, dialogH, dialogY + dialogTemp, image_offset_table(1))
            dialog_height = dialog_height + 16
        Else
            flag_trigger = flag_trigger + 1
            Call render_dialog_control(dialogH, dialogY)
        End If
        dialogTemp = 16
        If dialog_type = 0 Then
            dialogTemp = 18
        End If
        dialogH = dialogH + dialogTemp
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 0 Then
        scriptEntry = savedEntry
        continueFlag = -1
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 1 Then
        scriptEntry = increment_script_ip(scriptEntry)
        continueFlag = -1
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 2 Then
        If operand2 = 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            continueFlag = -1
            GoTo L_0041805E
        End If
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame = npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame + 1
        If npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame < operand2 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            continueFlag = -1
            GoTo L_0041805E
        End If
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame = 0
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 3 Then
        If operand2 = 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            GoTo L_004178B2
        End If
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame = npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame + 1
        If npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame < operand2 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            GoTo L_004178B2
        End If
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).scriptIdleFrame = 0
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 4 Then
        If operand2 > 0 Then
            tmp = operand2 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
            If (tmp > 0) And (tmp <= curr_scene_event_count) Then
                Call process_Script(operand1, tmp)
            End If
            GoTo L_0041805E
        End If
        Call process_Script(operand1, eventObjID)
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 5 Then
        Call check_trigger_flag()
        If mutex_can_change_palette = 0 Then
            sprite_frame_count = operand1
            If operand2 = 0 Then
                operand2 = 1
            End If
            If operand3 Then
                Call init_walk_frames()
            End If
            Call check_in_battle(operand2)
            sprite_frame_count = 0
            GoTo L_0041805E
        End If
        Call restore_screen()
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 6 Then
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        If CSng (operand1) < (rng * CSng (100)) Then
            If operand2 <> 0 Then
                scriptEntry = operand2
                GoTo L_004178B2
            End If
            continueFlag = -1
        End If
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 7 Then
        Call check_trigger_flag()
        battleResult = process_Battle(operand3, operand1)
        scriptEntry = increment_script_ip(scriptEntry)
        If (operand2 <> 0) And (battleResult = 1) Then
            scriptEntry = operand2
        End If
        If (operand3 <> 0) And (battleResult = 2) Then
            scriptEntry = operand3
        End If
        GoTo L_004178B2
    End If
    If tmp2 = 8 Then
        savedEntry = increment_script_ip(scriptEntry)
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 9 Then
        Call check_trigger_flag()
        If operand1 = 0 Then
            operand1 = 1
        End If
        For i = 1 To operand1
            If operand3 Then
                Call update_walk_frame()
                Call set_team_draw()
            End If
            Call process_event_objects(operand2)
            Call check_in_battle(1)
        Next
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 = 10 Then
        flag_trigger = 0
        flag_key_updown = 0
        i = -1
        While i < 0
            i = yes_no_menu(19, 0)
        Wend
        If i = 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1
            GoTo L_004178B2
        End If
        GoTo L_0041805E
    End If
    If tmp2 > 10 Then
        Call check_trigger_flag()
    End If
L_0041805E:
    If Not (continueFlag) Then
        Call process_scripts(operand3, operand2, operand1, opcode, scriptEntry, eventObjID)
        scriptEntry = increment_script_ip(scriptEntry)
        GoTo L_004178B2
    End If
L_00418098:
    Call check_trigger_flag()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub init_key_definitions()  'MethCallEngine entry
    tmp = VB40032.rtcGetTimer()
    Call PAL_GetKeyDefine(VB40032.rtcRandomize(tmp), key_scan_codes_global(0))

        key_scan_codes_global(4) = 200
    Else
        key_scan_codes_global(4) = 0

Sub select_item_with_filter(ByRef wordIdx, ByRef x, ByRef y)
    j = 0
    itemCount = 0
    menuX = 0
    rowCount = 3
    maxPage = 7
    k = 4
    tmp2 = 0
    menuY = rowCount - 1
    colCount = maxPage - 1
    maxScroll = compact_inventory()
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(256, battle_order_array(0))
    For rowPos = 0 To maxScroll
        battle_order_array(rowPos) = objects(inventory(rowPos).item).data6 And y
    Next
    colPos = maxScroll + 1
    If (x = 0) And (y = 1) Then
        For rowPos = 0 To RPG_team_number
            For l = 11 To 16
                prevCol = playerRoles(party(rowPos).role, l)
                If prevCol > 0 Then
                    If objects(prevCol).data6 And 1 Then
                        maxScroll = maxScroll + 1
                        battle_order_array(maxScroll) = 1
                        inventory(maxScroll).item = prevCol
                        inventory(maxScroll).amount = 1
                        inventory(maxScroll).amountInUse = 0
                    End If
                End If
            Next
        Next
    End If
    prevRow = ((maxScroll + menuY) \ rowCount) - k
    If prevRow < 0 Then
        prevRow = 0
    End If
    If wordIdx > maxScroll Then
        wordIdx = maxScroll
    End If
    tmp3 = wordIdx \ 3
