    prevScroll = wordIdx Mod 3
    If tmp3 > k Then
        tmp2 = tmp3 - k
        tmp3 = k
    End If
    m = 0
    If x = -1 Then
        bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(fire_mkf_data(0))
        screen_buffer_ptr = bufPtr
        Call PAL_PushScreen(fire_mkf_data(0))
        Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, fire_mkf_data(0), global_buf_1(64000))
    Else
        bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
        screen_buffer_ptr = bufPtr
        Call PAL_PushScreen(global_buf_1(0))
        Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, global_buf_1(0), global_buf_1(64000))
    End If
    highlightColor = -2
    While highlightColor = -2
        Call draw_menu_table(0, maxPage, 18, 9, m, 2)
        If tmp3 < 0 Then
            tmp3 = 0
        End If
        If tmp2 < 0 Then
            tmp2 = 0
        End If
        If tmp2 > prevRow Then
            tmp2 = prevRow
        End If
        If tmp2 > 0 Then
            If tmp3 < k Then
                tmp3 = k
                tmp2 = tmp2 - 1
            End If
        End If
        If tmp2 < prevRow Then
            If tmp3 > k Then
                tmp3 = k
                tmp2 = tmp2 + 1
            End If
            GoTo L_00418DBA
        End If
        If tmp3 > colCount Then
            tmp3 = colCount
        End If
L_00418DBA:
        If prevScroll < 0 Then
            prevScroll = 0
        End If
        If prevScroll > menuY Then
            prevScroll = menuY
        End If
        wordIdx = ((tmp2 + tmp3) * rowCount) + prevScroll
        If wordIdx > maxScroll Then
            prevScroll = j
            tmp3 = itemCount
            tmp2 = menuX
            wordIdx = ((tmp2 + tmp3) * rowCount) + prevScroll
        End If
        invIdx = m + 12
        o = tmp2 * rowCount
        For n = 0 To colCount
            i = 16
            For tmp4 = 0 To menuY
                inputResult = inventory(o).amount - inventory(o).amountInUse
                If battle_order_array(o) And (inputResult > 0) Then
                    If (o = wordIdx) And (highlightColor = -2) Then
                        tmp5 = 250
                        GoTo L_00418F3C
                    End If
                    tmp5 = 78
                    If o >= colPos Then
                        tmp5 = 200
                    End If
                    GoTo L_00418F3C
                End If
                If (o = wordIdx) And (highlightColor = -2) Then
                    tmp5 = 28
                    GoTo L_00418F3C
                End If
                tmp5 = 24
L_00418F3C:
                If inventory(o).amount > 0 Then
                    Call draw_text_at(tmp5, inventory(o).item, invIdx, i)
                End If
                If inputResult > 1 Then
                    Call display_number(0, inputResult, invIdx + 6, i + 84)
                End If
                If o = wordIdx Then
                    Call make_dialog_frame(invIdx + 11, i + 24)
                End If
                i = i + 100
                o = o + 1
            Next
            invIdx = invIdx + 18
        Next
        If x = 1 Then
            If battle_order_array(wordIdx) Then
                Call draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(0, objects(inventory(wordIdx).item).data1 \ 2, 150, 224, 25)
            Else
                Call frame_menu(0, 5, 150, 224)
            End If
        End If
        Call draw_object_icon(inventory(wordIdx).item, 140, 0)
        j = prevScroll
        itemCount = tmp3
        menuX = tmp2
        If x = -1 Then
            Call PAL_PopScreen(fire_mkf_data(0))
        Else
            Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
        End If
        If x <= 0 Then
            tmp7 = screen_buffer_ptr
            screen_buffer_ptr = 0
            Call process_Script(objects(inventory(wordIdx).item).data5, 151)
            screen_buffer_ptr = tmp7
        End If
        tmp5 = read_key()
        If tmp5 = 1 Then
            highlightColor = -1
        End If
        If battle_order_array(wordIdx) And (inventory(wordIdx).amount > inventory(wordIdx).amountInUse) Then
            If tmp5 = 2 Then
                highlightColor = wordIdx
            End If
        End If
        If tmp5 = 3 Then
            tmp3 = tmp3 - 1
        End If
        If tmp5 = 4 Then
            tmp3 = tmp3 + 1
        End If
        If tmp5 = 5 Then
            prevScroll = prevScroll - 1
        End If
        If tmp5 = 6 Then
            prevScroll = prevScroll + 1
        End If
        If tmp5 = 7 Then
            tmp2 = tmp2 - 5
        End If
        If tmp5 = 8 Then
            tmp2 = tmp2 + 5
        End If
    Wend
    screen_buffer_ptr = 0
    If highlightColor >= 0 Then
        highlightColor = inventory(highlightColor).item
    End If
    For rowPos = colPos To maxScroll
        inventory(rowPos).amount = 0
        inventory(rowPos).item = 0
    Next
    tmp = highlightColor
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub enemy_physical_attack(ByRef enemyIdx, ByRef targetIdx, ByRef targetRole, ByRef useMagic)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(20, battle_order_array(0))
    attackerRole = party(targetIdx).role
    atkPower = get_player_attribute_total(19, attackerRole)
    If player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = 3 Then
        atkPower = atkPower + atkPower
    End If
    battle_order_array(targetIdx) = -1
    rngVal = -VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * 0.85
    If check_player_alive(targetIdx) <= 1 Then
        If rngVal = -1 Then
            rngVal = 1
            hitChance = -1
            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                If party(i).role = playerRoles(attackerRole, 31) Then
                    hitChance = i
                End If
            Next
            If hitChance >= 0 Then
                If check_player_alive(hitChance) > 1 Then
                    rngVal = -hitChance + 10
                End If
            End If
        Else
            rngVal = 1
        End If
    End If
    battle_order_array(targetIdx + 10) = rngVal
    idleAnimSpeed = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackSound
    If enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magic = 0 Then
        If useMagic Then
            idleAnimSpeed = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicSound
        End If
    End If
    Call play_sound(0, idleAnimSpeed)
    If enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames > 0 Then
        attack_done_flag = 0
        For i = 1 To enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames
            enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = (enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames + i) - 1
            Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
        Next
    End If
    For i = 1 To (3 - enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames)
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x - 2
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y - 1
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Next
    Call play_sound(0, enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).actionSound)
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    attack_done_flag = 0
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x - 44
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y - 16
    j = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).callSound
    If rngVal = -1 Then
        player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = 3
        j = playerRoles(party(targetIdx).role, 73)
    End If
    If rngVal <= -10 Then
        hitChance = Abs (rngVal + 10)
        player_battle_sprite(hitChance).direction = 3
        j = playerRoles(party(hitChance).role, 73)
        player_battle_sprite(hitChance).x = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x - 24
        player_battle_sprite(hitChance).y = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y - 12
    End If
    If enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackFrames = 0 Then
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames - 1
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 3)
    Else
        For i = 1 To enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackFrames
            enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = ((enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames) + i) - 1
            Call draw_battle_scene(0, enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).actWaitFrames)
        Next
    End If
    If rngVal = 0 Then
        player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = 4
    End If
    If rngVal >= 0 Then
        enemy_battle_data_ext(targetIdx) = -1
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        damage = calc_battle_damage(atkPower, CSng (targetRole) + rng)
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        damage = (CSng (damage) * (CSng (1) + (rng / CSng (8)))) + rng2
        If player_status(targetIdx, 6) > 0 Then
            damage = damage \ 2
        End If
        If damage > playerRoles(attackerRole, 9) Then
            damage = playerRoles(attackerRole, 9)
        End If
        playerRoles(attackerRole, 9) = playerRoles(attackerRole, 9) - damage
        Call add_damage_number(1, damage, player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y - 70, player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x)
    End If
    Call play_sound(0, j)
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    If rngVal > -10 Then
        player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x + 8
        player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y + 4
    Else
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x - 10
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y - 8
        player_battle_sprite(hitChance).x = player_battle_sprite(hitChance).x + 4
        player_battle_sprite(hitChance).y = player_battle_sprite(hitChance).y + 2
    End If
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    If playerRoles(attackerRole, 9) <= 0 Then
        Call play_sound(1, playerRoles(attackerRole, 68))
    End If
    player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).x + 2
    player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).y + 1
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 3)
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).origX
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).origY
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = 0
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).direction = player_battle_sprite(targetIdx).origDirection
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    attack_done_flag = -1
    rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
    targetIdx = get_player_attribute_total(22, party(targetIdx).role)
    If ((rngVal >= 0) And (CSng (enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackEquivItemRate) > (rng2 * CSng (10)))) And ((VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (100)) > CSng (targetIdx)) Then
        Call process_Script(objects(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackEquivItem).data2, targetIdx)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub calc_display_theurgy(ByRef enemyIdx, ByRef targetRole, ByRef itemID, ByRef power)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(25, battle_order_array(0))
    itemObj = objects(itemID).data0
    Call copy_subfile_data(itemObj)
    If theurgy_data(0).effect < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    actionType = theurgy_data(0).baseDamage
    If enemyIdx <= enemy_pos_count Then
        Call load_theurgy_image(itemObj)
        If theurgy_data(0).fireDelay = 0 Then
            player_battle_sprite(enemyIdx).direction = 6
        End If
    End If
    If enemyIdx = 9 Then
        itemObj = theurgy_data(0).effect
        Call copy_subfile_data(itemObj)
    End If
    attack_done_flag = 0
    If theurgy_data(0).type < 4 Then
        If theurgy_data(0).type > 0 Then
            For i = 0 To team_number
                If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
                    battle_order_array(i) = -1
                End If
            Next
        End If
    End If
    effectIdx = theurgy_data(0).type
    Select Case effectIdx
        Case 7
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = 160 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = 200 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
        Case 6
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = 240 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = 160 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
        Case 5
            effect_particle_count = 0
            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                effect_particle_count = effect_particle_count + 1
                effect_x_coords(effect_particle_count) = player_battle_sprite(i).x + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
                effect_y_coords(effect_particle_count) = player_battle_sprite(i).y + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
                effect_frames(effect_particle_count) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
                battle_order_array(i) = -1
            Next
        Case 4
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(targetRole).x + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(targetRole).y + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
            battle_order_array(targetRole) = -1
        Case 3
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = 160 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = 200 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
        Case 2
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = 120 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = 100 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
        Case 1
            effect_particle_count = 3
            effect_x_coords(1) = 70
            effect_y_coords(1) = 140
            effect_x_coords(2) = 100
            effect_y_coords(2) = 110
            effect_x_coords(3) = 160
            effect_y_coords(3) = 100
            For i = 1 To effect_particle_count
                effect_x_coords(i) = effect_x_coords(i) + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
                effect_y_coords(i) = effect_y_coords(i) + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
                effect_frames(i) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
            Next
        Case 0
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = enemy_battle_data(targetRole).x + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = enemy_battle_data(targetRole).y + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
            battle_order_array(targetRole) = -1
    End Select
    effect_frame_count = 0
    targetRole = enemyIdx
    If theurgy_data(0).fireDelay > 0 Then
        If enemyIdx < 9 Then
            Call play_sound(0, theurgy_data(0).sound)
        End If
        For l = 0 To (theurgy_data(0).fireDelay - 1)
            Call draw_battle_scene(theurgy_data(0).speed, 1)
            effect_frame_count
        Next
        player_battle_sprite(enemyIdx).direction = 6
        targetRole = 10
    End If
    Call play_theurgy_anim(targetRole, effect_frame_count, itemObj)
    effect_particle_count = 0
    blow_away_flag = 0
    If theurgy_data(0).type <= 3 Then
        For j = 0 To team_number
            rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
            damage = power + ((CSng (power) * rng) \ 10)
            If battle_order_array(j) Then
                attrIdx = theurgy_data(0).elemental
                baseDmg = calc_sprite_y_offset(4, j) + enemy_runtime_data(j).defense
                If battle_order_array(j) Then
                    damage = (calc_battle_damage(baseDmg, damage) \ 2) + actionType
                    If (attrIdx > 0) And (attrIdx < 7) Then
                        resistVal = enemy_runtime_data(j).elemResistance0
                        If attrIdx = 2 Then
                            resistVal = enemy_runtime_data(j).elemResistance1
                        End If
                        If attrIdx = 3 Then
                            resistVal = enemy_runtime_data(j).elemResistance2
                        End If
                        If attrIdx = 4 Then
                            resistVal = enemy_runtime_data(j).elemResistance3
                        End If
                        If attrIdx = 5 Then
                            resistVal = enemy_runtime_data(j).elemResistance4
                        End If
                        If attrIdx = 6 Then
                            resistVal = enemy_runtime_data(j).poisonResistance
                        End If
                        damage = CSng (damage) * (CSng (10 - resistVal) / CSng (5))
                    End If
                End If
                damage = calc_magic_damage(theurgy_data(0).elemental, damage)
                If damage < 0 Then
                    damage = 0
                End If
                battle_order_array(20 + j) = damage
            End If
        Next
        GoTo L_0041A83C
    End If
    Call draw_battle_scene(-4, 1)
    If theurgy_data(0).type <= 7 Then
        For k = 2 To 16
            Call PAL_WaitTime(4)
            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                If battle_order_array(i) Then
                    Call get_sprite_frame_data(10 - Abs (k - 10), i)
                End If
            Next
        Next
    End If
L_0041A83C:
    attack_done_flag = -1
    battle_dest_x = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub Sub_Main()
    initResult = <unknown>(comTmp, 127)
    comTmp2 = initResult
    exit_flag = 0
L_0041A8CE:
    initResult = PAL_InitCD()
    comTmp2 = initResult
    If comTmp2 Then
        tmp20 = 'Error'
        tmp10 = '請放入 仙劍奇俠傳 for Windows 95 CD-ROM!'
        comTmp2 = VB40032.rtcMsgBox(tmp20, 1, tmp10)
        If comTmp2 = 1 Then
            GoTo L_0041A8CE
        Else
            End
        End If
    End If
    Call PAL_SetTimer(Me.method_0268(Me.method_0028(vb_com_object_1.f0000, vb_com_object_2.f0000), 0, vb_com_object_1.f0000), DoEvents_check_exit())
    dsInitResult = PAL_InitDSound(Me.method_0038(initResult, vb_com_object_1.f0000), initResult)
    If dsInitResult <> 0 Then
        music_mode = 0
        has_sfx_raw = 0
    Else
        music_mode = 2
        has_sfx_raw = 1
    End If
    use_cd_flag = music_mode <> 0
    has_sfx = has_sfx_raw <> 0
    initResult = PAL_InitInput(Me.method_0038(Me.method_000C(load_sound_data(), diTmp, vb_com_object_2.f0000), diTmp, dsInitResult, vb_com_object_1.f0000), dsInitResult, dsResult)
    comTmp2 = initResult
    If comTmp2 Then
        Call PAL_KillTimer(Me.method_002C(vb_com_object_1.f0000, vb_com_object_2.f0000))
        tmp20 = 'Error'
        dsResult = 'DirectInput Initialize Error!'
        comTmp2 = VB40032.rtcMsgBox(tmp20, 0, dsResult)
        End
    End If
    tmp5 = 'M.MSG'
    fh_M_MSG = open_file(0, tmp5)
    tmp5 = 'RNG.MKF'
    fh_RNG_MKF = open_file(0, tmp5)
    tmp5 = 'MGO.MKF'
    fh_MGO_MKF = open_file(0, tmp5)
    tmp5 = 'F.MKF'
    fh_F_MKF = open_file(0, tmp5)
    tmp5 = 'ABC.MKF'
    fh_ABC_MKF = open_file(0, tmp5)
    ddInitResult = user32.SetMode(dsInitResult, Me.method_0038(Load_system_files(), dsInitResult, vb_com_object_1.f0000))
    comTmp2 = ddInitResult
    If comTmp2 Then
        tmp20 = 'Error'
        dsResult = 'DirectDraw Initialize Error!'
        comTmp2 = VB40032.rtcMsgBox(tmp20, 0, dsResult)
        Call PAL_KillTimer(Me.method_002C(vb_com_object_1.f0000, vb_com_object_2.f0000))
        Call PAL_ResetMode()
        Call PAL_ShutdownDSound()
        End
    End If
    Call winmm.ShowCursor(0)
    Call PAL_ClearScreen(Me.method_0044(0, vb_com_object_1.f0000), DoEvents_check_exit())
    init_done_flag = 1
    Call PAL_PlayAvi(Me.method_0038(dsInitResult, vb_com_object_1.f0000), 0, 1, dsInitResult)
    Call PAL_PlayAvi(Me.method_0038(dsInitResult, vb_com_object_1.f0000), 0, 2, dsInitResult)
    palette_fade_active = 0
    Call fade_in(2)
    RPG_ememy_chase_rate = 1
    RPG_change_chaserate_times = 0
    music_mode_init = 3
    Call read_palette(0)
    Call play_all_kinds_music(1, 4)
L_0041AED4:
    Call show_fbp_picture(0, 2)
    Call fade_out_palette(1)
    battle_order_array(0) = 7
    battle_order_array(1) = 8
    battle_order_array(100) = -1
    battle_order_array(101) = -1
    menuParam = 0
    comTmp3 = menu_select(2, -1, 84, 112, menuParam)
    redraw_flag = 16
    If comTmp3 = 1 Then
        tmp = check_save_file()
        If tmp < 0 Then
            GoTo L_0041AED4
        End If
        Call LoadRPG_internal(tmp + 1)
        If RPG_save_number = 0 Then
        Else
            redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 2
        End If
    Else
        Call PAL_PlayAvi(Me.method_0038(midi_close(), fade_in(1), dsInitResult, vb_com_object_1.f0000), 0, 3, dsInitResult)
        scene_to_load = 1
        redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 13
        For tmp4 = 0 To 4
            For result = 0 To 7
                If result > 0 Then
                    result2 = (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (2)) + CSng (2)
                    j = VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (20)
                Else
                    result2 = 0
                    j = 0
                End If
                playerExp(tmp4, result).level = playerRoles(tmp4, 6) + result2
                playerExp(tmp4, result).exp = j
            Next
        Next
    End If
    scrollStepX = 16
    scrollStepY = 8
    frame_counter = 0
    battle_viewport_x = (63 - 10) * 32
    battle_viewport_y = (127 - 12) * 16
L_0041B14A:
    Call scene_transition()
    Do
        keyPressed = 0
        Call read_direction_input(scene_leave_flag, scene_enter_flag)
        keyPressed = key_pressed
        redraw_flag = 0
        Call process_event_objects(-1)
        If redraw_flag Then
            GoTo L_0041B14A
        End If
        Call PAL_ClearMenu(8192, global_buf_2(0))
        Call PAL_ClearTree()
        If scene_enter_flag = 0 Then
            scene_enter_flag = -scene_leave_flag
        End If
        If scene_leave_flag = 0 Then
            scene_leave_flag = scene_enter_flag
        End If
        scrollDeltaX = 0
        scrollDeltaY = 0
        If scene_enter_flag <> 0 Then
            If load_map_data(0, (team_abstract_y + RPG_viewport_y) + (scene_leave_flag * 8), (team_abstract_x + RPG_viewport_x) + (scene_enter_flag * 16)) Then
                scrollDeltaX = scene_enter_flag * scrollStepX
                scrollDeltaY = scene_leave_flag * scrollStepY
                scrollMode = 1
            End If
        End If
        Call PAL_ExTF(scene_leave_flag, scene_enter_flag, RPG_team_direction)
        x_off = RPG_viewport_x + team_abstract_x
        y_off = RPG_viewport_y + team_abstract_y
        viewport_x_bak = RPG_viewport_x
        viewport_y_bak = RPG_viewport_y
        If scrollMode > 0 Then
            scrollMode = scrollMode - 1
            RPG_viewport_x = RPG_viewport_x + scrollDeltaX
            RPG_viewport_y = RPG_viewport_y + scrollDeltaY
            Call PAL_Ffxy(battle_viewport_y, battle_viewport_x, RPG_viewport_y, RPG_viewport_x)
            If RPG_viewport_x = viewport_x_bak Then
                RPG_viewport_y = viewport_y_bak
            End If
            If RPG_viewport_y = viewport_y_bak Then
                RPG_viewport_x = viewport_x_bak
            End If
            Call update_party_position()
        Else
            Call init_walk_frames()
        End If
        party_abs_x = team_abstract_x + RPG_viewport_x
        party_abs_y = team_abstract_y + RPG_viewport_y
        Call get_party_role_id()
        Call get_sprites_curr_scene()
        Call render_scene_with_rng()
        Call update_viewport_scroll()
        Call render_scene_and_fade(1)
        tmp21 = keyPressed
        Select Case tmp21
            Case 1
                Call process_menu()
            Case 2
                Call check_trigger_events()
            Case 12
                Call select_magic()
                Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
            Case 13
                Call inventory_use_menu()
                Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
            Case 14
                tmp = 0
                comTmp3 = yes_no_menu(19, tmp)
                If comTmp3 = 1 Then
                    Call release_resources_exit()
                End If
                Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
            Case 15
                Call entry_stub_show_text()
                Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
            Case 16
                Call use_item_menu()
                Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
        End Select
        mutex_shaking = mutex_shaking Xor 1
    Loop While redraw_flag = 0
    GoTo L_0041B14A
End Sub

Sub enemy_magical_attack(ByRef enemyIdx, ByRef targetRole, ByRef itemID, ByRef power)
    i = 0
    itemObj = objects(itemID).data0
    Call copy_subfile_data(itemObj)
    attack_done_flag = 0
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x + 12
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y + 6
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x + 4
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y + 2
    Call draw_battle_scene(-4, 1)
    targetStart = (enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames) - 1
    targetEnd = ((enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames) + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackFrames) - 1
    spriteIdx = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicSound
    If spriteIdx < 0 Then
        spriteIdx = -spriteIdx
        i = 128
    End If
    Call play_sound(0, spriteIdx)
    Call enemy_cast_anim(enemyIdx)
    Call load_theurgy_image(itemObj)
    If battle_sub_flag = 0 Then
        Call PAL_WaitTime(20)
    End If
    If theurgy_data(0).fireDelay = 0 Then
        For j = targetStart To targetEnd
            enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = j
            Call draw_battle_scene(0, enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).actWaitFrames)
        Next
    End If
    effectIdx = theurgy_data(0).type
    Select Case effectIdx
        Case 3
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = 160 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = 200 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
        Case 2
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = 240 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = 150 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
        Case 1
            effect_particle_count = 3
            effect_x_coords(1) = 180
            effect_y_coords(1) = 180
            effect_x_coords(2) = 234
            effect_y_coords(2) = 170
            effect_x_coords(3) = 270
            effect_y_coords(3) = 146
            For j = 1 To effect_particle_count
                effect_x_coords(j) = effect_x_coords(j) + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
                effect_y_coords(j) = effect_y_coords(j) + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
                effect_frames(j) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
            Next
        Case 0
            effect_particle_count = 1
            effect_x_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(targetRole).x + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
            effect_y_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(targetRole).y + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
            effect_frames(1) = theurgy_data(0).layerOffset
    End Select
    Call process_Script(objects(itemID).data3, targetRole)
    effect_frame_count = 0
    If theurgy_data(0).fireDelay > 0 Then
        Call play_sound(0, theurgy_data(0).sound)
        i = 10
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = targetStart
        For l = 0 To (theurgy_data(0).fireDelay - 1)
            Call draw_battle_scene(theurgy_data(0).speed, 1)
            effect_frame_count
        Next
        For j = (targetStart + 1) To targetEnd
            enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = j
            Call draw_battle_scene(0, enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).actWaitFrames)
        Next
    End If
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(20, battle_order_array(0))
    If theurgy_data(0).type > 0 Then
        For j = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            targetRole = party(j).role
            battle_order_array(10 + j) = 1
            battle_order_array(j) = 0
            If playerRoles(targetRole, 9) > 0 Then
                If player_battle_sprite(j).direction = 3 Then
                    battle_order_array(10 + j) = 2
                End If
                If player_status(j, 6) > 0 Then
                    battle_order_array(10 + j) = battle_order_array(10 + j) * 2
                End If
                battle_order_array(j) = -1
                damageMult = -VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * 0.75
                If check_player_alive(j) < 2 Then
                    damageMult = 1
                End If
                If damageMult = -1 Then
                    player_battle_sprite(j).direction = 3
                    battle_order_array(10 + j) = battle_order_array(10 + j) + 1
                End If
            End If
        Next
        GoTo L_0041BE8C
    End If
    battle_order_array(10 + targetRole) = 1
    battle_order_array(targetRole) = -1
    If player_battle_sprite(targetRole).direction = 3 Then
        battle_order_array(10 + targetRole) = 2
    End If
    If player_status(targetRole, 6) > 0 Then
        battle_order_array(10 + targetRole) = battle_order_array(10 + targetRole) * 2
    End If
    damageMult = -VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * 0.75
    If (player_battle_sprite(targetRole).direction = 1) Or ((player_status(targetRole, 2) + player_status(targetRole, 1)) > 0) Then
        damageMult = 1
    End If
    If damageMult = -1 Then
        player_battle_sprite(targetRole).direction = 3
        battle_order_array(10 + targetRole) = battle_order_array(10 + targetRole) + 1
    End If
L_0041BE8C:
    Call play_theurgy_anim(i, effect_frame_count, itemObj)
    effect_particle_count = 0
    For k = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        targetRole = party(k).role
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        baseDamage = CSng (power) + (rng * CSng (4))
        If battle_order_array(k) Then
            targetStart = theurgy_data(0).elemental
            targetIdx = get_player_attribute_total(19, targetRole)
            baseDamage = (calc_battle_damage(targetIdx, baseDamage) \ 2) + theurgy_data(0).baseDamage
            baseDamage = baseDamage \ battle_order_array(10 + k)
            If (targetStart > 0) And (targetStart < 7) Then
                If targetStart = 6 Then
                    targetStart = 0
                End If
                theurgyIdx = get_player_attribute_total(22 + targetStart, targetRole)
                baseDamage = CSng (baseDamage) * (CSng (100 - theurgyIdx) / CSng (100))
            End If
            baseDamage = calc_magic_damage(theurgy_data(0).elemental, baseDamage)
            If baseDamage > playerRoles(targetRole, 9) Then
                baseDamage = playerRoles(targetRole, 9)
            End If
            If baseDamage < 0 Then
                baseDamage = 0
            End If
            playerRoles(targetRole, 9) = playerRoles(targetRole, 9) - baseDamage
            If playerRoles(targetRole, 9) <= 0 Then
                Call play_sound(1, playerRoles(targetRole, 68))
            End If
            Call add_damage_number(1, baseDamage, player_battle_sprite(k).y - 70, player_battle_sprite(k).x)
        End If
    Next
    frameDelay = 16
    For j = 0 To 5
        For k = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            If battle_order_array(k) Then
                If battle_order_array(k + 10) < 4 Then
                    player_battle_sprite(k).direction = 4
                    If j > 0 Then
                        player_battle_sprite(k).x = player_battle_sprite(k).x + frameDelay
                        player_battle_sprite(k).y = player_battle_sprite(k).y + (frameDelay \ 2)
                    End If
                End If
                If j < 3 Then
                    enemy_battle_data_ext(k) = -1
                End If
            End If
        Next
        frameDelay = frameDelay \ 2
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    Next
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = 0
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).x = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).origX
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).y = enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).origY
    Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    For k = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        If battle_order_array(k) Then
            player_battle_sprite(k).direction = player_battle_sprite(k).origDirection
        End If
    Next
    attack_done_flag = -1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub process_Battle(ByRef battleScene, ByRef surpriseFlag)
    battle_param = surpriseFlag
    For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
        k = party(i).role
        If playerRoles(k, 9) <= 0 Then
            playerRoles(k, 9) = 1
        End If
    Next
    curr_scene_id_cache = 0
    flag_battling = -1
    Call PAL_ClearTree(init_battle_state())
    For i = 0 To 11
        damage_numbers(i).timer = 0
    Next
    For i = 0 To 4
        For j = 0 To 7
            playerExp(i, j).count = 0
        Next
    Next
    battleResult = 0
    magic_select_idx = 0
    fade_step_count = 0
    dialog_y_pos = 200
    effect_sub_count = 0
    effect_particle_count = 0
    npc_dir_frame = 0
    battle_role_idx_2 = 0
    battle_select_max = 0
    battle_action_param = 0
    enemy_max_id = 0
    battle_max_hp_sum = 0
    battle_action_type = 0
    Call PAL_CopyMem(10, data_enemy_team(battleScene * 10), save_temp_buf(0))
    For i = 0 To 4
        enemy_battle_data(i).hp = 0
        enemyObjID = save_temp_buf(i)
        If enemyObjID > 0 Then
            Call load_enemy_data(enemyObjID, enemy_max_id)
        End If
        If enemyObjID >= 0 Then
            enemy_max_id = enemy_max_id + 1
        End If
    Next
    team_number = enemy_max_id - 1
    If enemy_max_id <= 0 Then
        GoTo L_00420C0E
    End If
    Call load_enemy_sprites()
    Call load_battle_sprites()
    If battle_extra_param <= 1 Then
        player_battle_sprite(0).x = 240
        player_battle_sprite(0).y = 170
    End If
    If battle_extra_param = 2 Then
        player_battle_sprite(0).x = 200
        player_battle_sprite(0).y = 176
        player_battle_sprite(1).x = 256
        player_battle_sprite(1).y = 152
    End If
    If battle_extra_param = 3 Then
        player_battle_sprite(0).x = 180
        player_battle_sprite(0).y = 180
        player_battle_sprite(1).x = 234
        player_battle_sprite(1).y = 170
        player_battle_sprite(2).x = 270
        player_battle_sprite(2).y = 146
    End If
    For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        player_battle_sprite(i).origX = player_battle_sprite(i).x
        player_battle_sprite(i).origY = player_battle_sprite(i).y
    Next
    Call update_player_battle_status()
    If RPG_battle_music_number > 0 Then
        Call play_all_kinds_music(1, RPG_battle_music_number)
    End If
    Call PAL_CopyMem(2, data_battlefield(RPG_battle_scene_number * 12), theurgy_effect_count)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(load_fbp_subfile(RPG_battle_scene_number), 64000, global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0))
    Call draw_enemy_battle_frame()
    For i = 0 To 5
        Call PAL_PopScreen6(rng_anim_frames(i), global_buf_1(0))
        Call PAL_WaitTime(6)
    Next
    Call PAL_CopyMem(fade_out_palette(0), 64000, bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0))
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(5, battle_enemy_data_ext(0))
    Do
        Call PAL_CopyMem(menu_bg_size, menu_bg_data(0), word_glyph_index(0))
        For i = 0 To team_number
            If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
                Call process_Script(enemy_battle_data(i).useScript, i)
            End If
        Next
        If fade_step_count Then
            battleResult = fade_step_count
            GoTo L_00420C0E
        End If
        If check_party_alive() = 0 Then
            battleResult = 1
            GoTo L_00420C0E
        End If
        If count_alive_enemies() = 0 Then
            battleResult = 3
            GoTo L_00420C0E
        End If
        directionParam = 0
        actionParam = 0
        battle_curr_role_idx = 0
        menuResult = 0
        For i = 0 To 255
            inventory(i).amountInUse = 0
        Next
        Call PAL_ClearMenu(40, battle_role_action(0).target)
        If auto_battle_flag = 0 Then
            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                player_battle_sprite(i).actionState = 0
            Next
            Call update_player_battle_status()
            Call draw_battle_status_bar()
        End If
L_0041DBEE:
        attack_done_flag = -1
        effect_particle_count = 0
        Do
            If battle_curr_role_idx > enemy_pos_count Then
                GoTo L_0041EAC0
            End If
            If player_status(battle_curr_role_idx, 4) Then
                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = random_enemy_id()
                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 0
                GoTo L_0041DD16
            End If
            If ((player_status(battle_curr_role_idx, 1) + player_status(battle_curr_role_idx, 2)) + player_status(battle_curr_role_idx, 0)) = 0 Then
                If playerRoles(party(battle_curr_role_idx).role, 9) > 0 Then
                    GoTo L_0041DD2A
                End If
                GoTo L_0041DD16
            End If
            Call draw_battle_scene(0, 4)
L_0041DD16:
            battle_curr_role_idx = battle_curr_role_idx + 1
        Loop
L_0041DD2A:
        For i = battle_curr_role_idx To enemy_pos_count
            If (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 3) Or (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 4) Then
                If inventory(battle_role_action(i).invIndex).amountInUse > 0 Then
                    inventory(battle_role_action(i).invIndex).amountInUse = inventory(battle_role_action(i).invIndex).amountInUse - 1
                End If
                battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0
            End If
        Next
        If auto_battle_flag = 0 Then
            battle_target_cursor = -1
            multi_event_param_3 = -1
            Do
                battle_role_idx_2 = -1
                Call PAL_ClearMenu(4, battle_order_array(0))
                If (enemy_pos_count = 0) Or (player_battle_sprite(battle_curr_role_idx).direction <> 0) Then
                    battle_order_array(2) = -1
                End If
                If player_status(battle_curr_role_idx, 3) Then
                    battle_order_array(1) = -1
                End If
                dirResult = select_direction_menu(directionParam)
                If dirResult > -9 Then
                    If dirResult = -1 Then
                        prevRoleIdx = battle_curr_role_idx
                        For i = 0 To (battle_curr_role_idx - 1)
                            If check_player_alive(i) > 0 Then
                                prevRoleIdx = i
                            End If
                        Next
                        battle_curr_role_idx = prevRoleIdx
                        GoTo L_0041DBEE
                    End If
                    tmp2 = dirResult
                    If tmp2 = 0 Then
                        If playerRoles(party(battle_curr_role_idx).role, 4) Then
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 10
                            Call check_battle_action(actionParam, directionParam)
                            GoTo L_0041E404
                        End If
                        enemyTarget = select_enemy_target()
                        If enemyTarget >= 0 Then
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = enemyTarget
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 0
                            Call check_battle_action(actionParam, directionParam)
                        End If
                        GoTo L_0041E404
                    End If
                    If tmp2 = 1 Then
L_0041DFF8:
                        If player_status(battle_curr_role_idx, 3) Then
                            thyrgyType = 0
                        Else
                            thyrgyType = 2
                        End If
                        thyrgyResult3 = select_thurgy(thyrgyType, actionParam, party(battle_curr_role_idx).role)
                        Call draw_battle_status_bar()
                        If thyrgyResult3 >= 0 Then
                            Call PAL_GetBin(3, objects(thyrgyResult3).data6, prevRoleIdx)
                            Call PAL_GetBin(4, objects(thyrgyResult3).data6, isTargeted)
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).itemID = thyrgyResult3
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).invIndex = actionParam
                            If prevRoleIdx Then
                                If isTargeted Then
                                    battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = battle_curr_role_idx
                                    GoTo L_0041E134
                                End If
                                enemyTarget = select_enemy_target()
                                If enemyTarget >= 0 Then
                                    battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = enemyTarget
                                    GoTo L_0041E134
                                End If
                                GoTo L_0041DFF8
L_0041E134:
                                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 2
                            Else
                                If isTargeted Then
                                    battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = battle_curr_role_idx
                                    GoTo L_0041E1AA
                                End If
                                thyrgyResult4 = select_battle_target()
                                If thyrgyResult4 >= 0 Then
                                    battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = thyrgyResult4
                                    GoTo L_0041E1AA
                                End If
                                GoTo L_0041DFF8
L_0041E1AA:
                                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 1
                            End If
                            Call check_battle_action(actionParam, directionParam)
                        End If
                        GoTo L_0041E404
                    End If
                    If tmp2 = 2 Then
                        Call PAL_GetBin(4, objects(player_battle_sprite(battle_curr_role_idx).equipID).data6, isTargeted)
                        If isTargeted = 0 Then
                            enemyTarget = select_enemy_target()
                        Else
                            enemyTarget = 0
                        End If
                        If enemyTarget >= 0 Then
                            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                                battle_role_action(i).actionType = -1
                            Next
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 7
                            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = enemyTarget
                            battle_curr_role_idx = enemy_pos_count
                            Call check_battle_action(actionParam, directionParam)
                        End If
                        GoTo L_0041E404
                    End If
                    If tmp2 = 3 Then
                        Do
                            thyrgyResult2 = menu_loop(5, 3, 56, 16, 4, menuParam1)
                            If thyrgyResult2 < 0 Then
                                Call draw_battle_status_bar()
                                GoTo L_0041E404
                            End If
                            tmp3 = thyrgyResult2
                            If tmp3 = 0 Then
                                thyrgyResult7 = set_auto_battle_targets()
                                GoTo L_0041E3D2
                            End If
                            If tmp3 = 1 Then
                                thyrgyResult = menu_loop(2, 2, 23, 50, 24, menuParam2)
                                If thyrgyResult < 0 Then
                                    thyrgyResult2 = -2
                                    GoTo L_0041E3D2
                                End If
                                thyrgyResult2 = select_battle_action(thyrgyResult)
                                GoTo L_0041E3D2
                            End If
                            If tmp3 = 2 Then
                                Call battle_set_action_code()
                                GoTo L_0041E3D2
                            End If
                            If tmp3 = 3 Then
                                Call battle_set_action_walk()
                                GoTo L_0041E3D2
                            End If
                            If tmp3 = 4 Then
                                Call entry_stub_show_text_and_dialog()
                            End If
L_0041E3D2:
                        Loop While thyrgyResult2 = -2
                        If thyrgyResult2 <> 4 Then
                            Call check_battle_action(actionParam, directionParam)
                            Call draw_battle_status_bar()
                        End If
                    End If
L_0041E404:
                    battle_role_idx_2 = 0
                    GoTo L_0041EAAA
                End If
                If dirResult = -9 Then
                    menuResult = -1
                    For i = battle_curr_role_idx To enemy_pos_count
                        CopyBytes battle_role_action(i), battle_role_data_copy(i), len=10
                        If (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 10) And (playerRoles(party(i).role, 4) = 0) Then
                            battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0
                        End If
                        If (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0) And (playerRoles(party(i).role, 4) <> 0) Then
                            battle_role_action(i).actionType = 10
                        End If
                        If (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 1) Or (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 2) Then
                            itemObjID = battle_role_action(i).itemID
                            k = objects(itemObjID).data0
                            Call copy_subfile_data(k)
                            menuResult2 = find_magic_index(itemObjID, i)
                            battle_role_action(i).invIndex = menuResult2 - 32
                            If (player_status(i, 3) Or (menuResult2 = 0)) Or (playerRoles(party(i).role, 10) < theurgy_data(0).costMP) Then
                                If battle_role_action(i).actionType = 1 Then
                                    battle_role_action(i).actionType = 5
                                Else
                                    battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                        If (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 3) Or (battle_role_action(i).actionType = 4) Then
                            menuResult2 = find_inventory_item(battle_role_action(i).itemID)
                            If menuResult2 >= 0 Then
                                battle_role_action(i).invIndex = menuResult2
                                If inventory(menuResult2).amount <= inventory(menuResult2).amountInUse Then
                                    If battle_role_action(i).actionType = 3 Then
                                        battle_role_action(i).actionType = 5
                                        GoTo L_0041E7AC
                                    End If
                                    battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0
                                    GoTo L_0041E7AC
                                End If
                                inventory(menuResult2).amountInUse = inventory(menuResult2).amountInUse + 1
                                GoTo L_0041E7AC
                            End If
                            If battle_role_action(i).actionType = 3 Then
                                battle_role_action(i).actionType = 5
                                GoTo L_0041E7AC
                            End If
                            battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0
                        End If
L_0041E7AC:
                    Next
                    battle_curr_role_idx = enemy_pos_count
                End If
                thyrgyResult2 = 0
                If dirResult = -10 Then
                    thyrgyResult7 = set_auto_battle_targets()
                End If
                If dirResult = -11 Then
                    Call battle_set_action_code()
                End If
                If dirResult = -12 Then
                    thyrgyResult2 = select_battle_action(0)
                End If
                If dirResult = -13 Then
                    thyrgyResult2 = select_battle_action(1)
                End If
                If dirResult = -14 Then
                    Call battle_set_action_walk()
                End If
                If dirResult = -15 Then
                    Call entry_stub_show_text_and_dialog()
                    GoTo L_0041DBEE
                End If
                If dirResult = -16 Then
                    For i = battle_curr_role_idx To enemy_pos_count
                        k = party(i).role
                        battle_role_action(i).target = random_enemy_id()
                        hasMagic = 0
                        maxDmg = 0
                        For j = 32 To 63
                            equipObjID = playerRoles(k, j)
                            If equipObjID > 0 Then
                                magicSubfile = objects(equipObjID).data0
                                Call copy_subfile_data(magicSubfile)
                                If ((playerRoles(k, 10) >= theurgy_data(0).costMP) And ((theurgy_data(0).type = 9) Or (theurgy_data(0).type <= 3))) And (theurgy_data(0).costMP > 1) Then
                                    rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
                                    floatVal = CSng (theurgy_data(0).baseDamage) + Int (rng2 * CSng (60))
                                    If floatVal > maxDmg Then
                                        maxDmg = floatVal
                                        hasMagic = equipObjID
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                        Next
                        If (hasMagic > 0) And (player_status(i, 3) <= 0) Then
                            battle_role_action(i).actionType = 2
                            battle_role_action(i).itemID = hasMagic
                        Else
                            battle_role_action(i).actionType = 0
                        End If
                    Next
                    battle_curr_role_idx = enemy_pos_count
                End If
            Loop While thyrgyResult2 = -2
            Call check_battle_action(actionParam, directionParam)
            Call draw_battle_status_bar()
L_0041EAAA:
            If battle_curr_role_idx < battle_extra_param Then
                GoTo L_0041DBEE
            End If
        End If
L_0041EAC0:
        dialog_y_pos = 200
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
        battle_action_queue = -1
        For o = 0 To team_number
            rng = Sgn (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (enemy_runtime_data(battle_enemy_idx).dualMove))
            rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
            prevRoleIdx = CSng (calc_sprite_y_offset(3, battle_enemy_idx) + enemy_runtime_data(battle_enemy_idx).dexterity) * (0.9 + (rng2 / CSng (5)))
            For i = 0 To rng
                battle_action_queue = battle_action_queue + 1
                battle_action_queue_ext(battle_action_queue) = 10 + battle_enemy_idx
                save_temp_buf(battle_action_queue) = prevRoleIdx
                battle_sprite_ext(battle_action_queue) = i
            Next
            battle_enemy_idx
        Next
        For p = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            If menuResult = 0 Then
                CopyBytes battle_role_data_copy(battle_enemy_idx), battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx), len=10
            End If
            actionType = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).actionType
            battle_action_queue = battle_action_queue + 1
            battle_action_queue_ext(battle_action_queue) = battle_enemy_idx
            rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
            prevRoleIdx = CSng (get_player_attribute_total(20, party(battle_enemy_idx).role)) * (0.9 + (rng2 / CSng (5)))
            If actionType = 7 Then
                prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx * 10
            End If
            If actionType = 5 Then
                prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx * 5
            End If
            If (actionType = 1) Or (actionType = 3) Then
                prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx * 3
            End If
            If actionType = 8 Then
                prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx \ 2
            End If
            If player_status(battle_enemy_idx, 7) > 0 Then
                prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx * 3
            End If
            If player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).direction = 1 Then
                prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx \ 2
            End If
            If player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).direction = 2 Then
                prevRoleIdx = 0
            End If
            save_temp_buf(battle_action_queue) = prevRoleIdx
            battle_enemy_idx
        Next
        For i = 0 To (battle_action_queue - 1)
            For j = (i + 1) To battle_action_queue
                If save_temp_buf(i) < save_temp_buf(j) Then
                    Call swap_values(save_temp_buf(j), save_temp_buf(i))
                    Call swap_values(battle_action_queue_ext(j), battle_action_queue_ext(i))
                    Call swap_values(battle_sprite_ext(j), battle_sprite_ext(i))
                End If
            Next
        Next
        For l = 0 To battle_action_queue
            npc_sprite_num = 0
            battle_sub_flag = 0
            If battle_action_queue_ext(l) >= 10 Then
                If battle_select_max = 0 Then
                    battle_enemy_idx = battle_action_queue_ext(l) - 10
                    If enemy_status(battle_enemy_idx, 2) Or enemy_status(battle_enemy_idx, 1) Then
                        GoTo L_00420322
                    End If
                    If l > 0 Then
                        battle_sub_flag = battle_action_queue_ext(l) = battle_action_queue_ext(l - 1)
                    End If
                    If enemy_battle_data(battle_enemy_idx).hp > 0 Then
                        thyrgyResult8 = random_alive_party_member()
                        Call enemy_attack_role(battle_sprite_ext(l), thyrgyResult8, battle_enemy_idx)
                    End If
                End If
                GoTo L_00420322
            End If
            battle_enemy_idx = battle_action_queue_ext(l)
            If battle_enemy_idx >= 0 Then
                roleID = party(battle_enemy_idx).role
                If (playerRoles(roleID, 9) > 0) Or (player_status(battle_enemy_idx, 4) > 0) Then
                    If player_status(battle_enemy_idx, 2) > 0 Then
                        GoTo L_00420322
                    End If
                    If player_status(battle_enemy_idx, 1) > 0 Then
                        GoTo L_00420322
                    End If
                    If auto_battle_flag >= 2 Then
                        battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target = thyrgyResult7
                        If auto_battle_flag = 2 Then
                            battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).actionType = 0
                        End If
                        If auto_battle_flag = 9 Then
                            battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).actionType = 2
                            rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
                            battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).itemID = playerRoles(roleID, CSng (32) + Int (rng2 * CSng (4)))
                        End If
                    End If
                    If player_status(battle_enemy_idx, 0) > 0 Then
                        battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).actionType = 9
                    End If
                    targetIdx = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target
                    If targetIdx >= 0 Then
                        While enemy_battle_data(targetIdx).hp <= 0
                            targetIdx = (targetIdx + 1) Mod enemy_max_id
                        Wend
                    Else
                        targetIdx = 0
                    End If
                    magic_select_idx = 0
                    in_battle_action = 0
                    battle_enemy_hp = 0
                    thyrgyResult5 = count_alive_enemies()
                    For i = 0 To 4
                        battle_enemy_data_ext(i) = enemy_battle_data(i).hp
                    Next
                    actionTypeSel = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).actionType
                    Select Case actionTypeSel
                        Case 0
                            Call calc_player_attack_damage(targetIdx, roleID, battle_enemy_idx)
                        Case 1
                            itemObjID = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).itemID
                            k = objects(itemObjID).data0
                            Call copy_subfile_data(k)
                            thyrgyResult6 = get_player_attribute_total(18, roleID)
                            If theurgy_data(0).type = 8 Then
                                hasEffect = 0
                            Else
                                hasEffect = 1
                            End If
                            Call player_attack_anim(hasEffect, battle_enemy_idx)
                            Call process_Script(objects(itemObjID).data3, battle_enemy_idx)
                            If redraw_hp_mp_flag Then
                                If theurgy_data(0).type = 8 Then
                                    Call play_sound(0, theurgy_data(0).sound)
                                    Call animate_battle_sprites(battle_enemy_idx, battle_enemy_idx)
                                End If
                                If theurgy_data(0).effect >= 0 Then
                                    Call calc_display_theurgy(thyrgyResult6, itemObjID, battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target, battle_enemy_idx)
                                End If
                                playerRoles(roleID, 10) = playerRoles(roleID, 10) - theurgy_data(0).costMP
                                GoSub L_00420B20
                                If theurgy_data(0).type = 8 Then
                                    player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).x = player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).origX
                                    player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).y = player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).origY
                                    Call load_battle_sprites()
                                    npc_sprite_num = -1
                                End If
                                rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
                                playerExp(roleID, 2).count = CSng (playerExp(roleID, 2).count) + (rng2 * CSng (2))
                                playerExp(roleID, 4).count = playerExp(roleID, 4).count + 1
                            End If
                        Case 2
                            itemObjID = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).itemID
                            k = objects(itemObjID).data0
                            Call copy_subfile_data(k)
                            mpCost = theurgy_data(0).costMP
                            If theurgy_data(0).type = 9 Then
                                hasEffect = 0
                            Else
                                hasEffect = 1
                            End If
                            Call player_attack_anim(hasEffect, battle_enemy_idx)
                            Call process_Script(objects(itemObjID).data3, battle_enemy_idx)
                            If redraw_hp_mp_flag Then
                                actionParam2 = battle_enemy_idx
                                npc_dir_frame = 0
                                thyrgyResult6 = get_player_attribute_total(18, roleID)
                                If theurgy_data(0).type = 9 Then
                                    tmp = k
                                    npc_dir_frame = -1
                                    Call cast_theurgy_anim(itemObjID)
                                    Call reset_battle_sprite_pos(battle_enemy_idx)
                                    actionParam2 = 9
                                End If
                                Call calc_display_theurgy(thyrgyResult6, itemObjID, targetIdx, actionParam2)
                                Call process_Script(objects(itemObjID).data2, targetIdx)
                                Call apply_enemy_poison_damage()
                                Call show_enemy_damage(thyrgyType)
                                If thyrgyType Then
                                    Call enemy_hit_reaction(5)
                                End If
                                playerRoles(roleID, 10) = playerRoles(roleID, 10) - mpCost
                                rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
                                playerExp(roleID, 2).count = CSng (playerExp(roleID, 2).count) + (rng2 * CSng (2))
                                playerExp(roleID, 4).count = playerExp(roleID, 4).count + 1
                            End If
                        Case 3
                            Call player_attack_anim(0, battle_enemy_idx)
                            itemObjID = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).itemID
                            Call draw_text_at(14, itemObjID, 50, 200)
                            Call play_sound(0, 28)
                            If battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target = -1 Then
                                targetStart = 0
                                targetEnd = enemy_pos_count
                                battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target = battle_enemy_idx
                            Else
                                targetStart = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target
                                targetEnd = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target
                            End If
                            For m = 2 To 16
                                For n = targetStart To targetEnd
                                    Call get_sprite_frame_data(10 - Abs (m - 10), n)
                                Next
                                Call PAL_WaitTime(5)
                            Next
                            GoSub L_00420B20
                            Call PAL_GetBin(3, objects(itemObjID).data6, prevRoleIdx)
                            If prevRoleIdx Then
                                Call remove_inventory_item(1, itemObjID)
                            End If
                        Case 4
                            itemObjID = battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).itemID
                            Call PAL_Delay(player_attack_anim(0, battle_enemy_idx), draw_text_at(14, itemObjID, 50, 200), 35)
                            player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).direction = 6
                            For i = 0 To 4
                                If party(i).role < 5 Then
                                    battle_role_data_ext(i).savedHP = playerRoles(party(i).role, 9)
                                    battle_role_data_ext(i).savedMP = playerRoles(party(i).role, 10)
                                End If
                            Next
                            Call play_sound(0, 27)
                            Call process_Script(objects(itemObjID).data4, targetIdx)
                            If redraw_hp_mp_flag Then
                                Call PAL_GetBin(3, objects(itemObjID).data6, prevRoleIdx)
                                If prevRoleIdx Then
                                    Call remove_inventory_item(1, itemObjID)
                                End If
                            End If
                            thyrgyType = 0
                            Call show_party_hp_mp_change(thyrgyType)
                            Call show_enemy_damage(thyrgyType)
                            If thyrgyType Then
                                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 3)
                                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 5)
                            End If
                            For i = 0 To team_number
                                If (battle_enemy_data_ext(i) > 0) And (enemy_battle_data(i).hp <= 0) Then
                                    npc_sprite_num = -1
                                    battle_enemy_hp = enemy_runtime_data(i).deathSound
                                End If
                            Next
                        Case 5
                            player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).direction = 3
                            player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).actionState = 3
                            Call draw_battle_scene(0, 4)
                            playerExp(roleID, 5).count = playerExp(roleID, 5).count + 2
                        Case 6
                            Call calc_player_attack_damage(targetIdx, roleID, battle_enemy_idx)
                            auto_battle_flag = 2
                        Case 7
                            equipID = player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).equipID
                            magicSubfile2 = objects(equipID).data0
                            Call copy_subfile_data(magicSubfile2)
                            thyrgyResult6 = 0
                            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                                k = party(i).role
                                If (check_player_alive(i) > 1) And (playerRoles(k, 9) >= theurgy_data(0).costMP) Then
                                    playerRoles(k, 9) = playerRoles(k, 9) - theurgy_data(0).costMP
                                    thyrgyResult6 = (thyrgyResult6 + playerRoles(k, 17)) + playerRoles(k, 18)
                                End If
                            Next
                            If theurgy_data(0).type < 9 Then
                                If enemy_pos_count >= 2 Then
                                    battle_sprite_data(0) = 274
                                    battle_sprite_data(1) = 244
                                    battle_sprite_data(2) = 216
                                    battle_sprite_data_ext(0) = 186
                                    battle_sprite_data_ext(1) = 172
                                    battle_sprite_data_ext(2) = 158
                                Else
                                    battle_sprite_data(0) = 244
                                    battle_sprite_data(1) = 216
                                    battle_sprite_data_ext(0) = 172
                                    battle_sprite_data_ext(1) = 158
                                End If
                                Call swap_values(battle_sprite_data(battle_enemy_idx), battle_sprite_data(enemy_pos_count))
                                Call swap_values(battle_sprite_data_ext(battle_enemy_idx), battle_sprite_data_ext(enemy_pos_count))
                                Call play_sound(0, 29)
                                Call adjust_battle_sprite_pos()
                                magic_select_idx = -1
                            End If
                            rng = 0
                            For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                                rng = rng - (check_player_alive(i) > 1)
                                battle_order_array(i) = i
                            Next
                            Call swap_values(battle_order_array(battle_enemy_idx), battle_order_array(enemy_pos_count))
                            If rng > 1 Then
                                If theurgy_data(0).type < 9 Then
                                    For k = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                                        i = battle_order_array(k)
                                        If check_player_alive(i) > 1 Then
                                            If i <> battle_enemy_idx Then
                                                player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 5
                                                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 3)
                                                player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 6
                                                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
                                            Else
                                                enemy_battle_data_ext(i) = -1
                                                player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 5
                                                Call draw_battle_scene(8, 1)
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Next
                                Else
                                    Call player_attack_anim(0, battle_enemy_idx)
                                End If
                                Call load_theurgy_image(magicSubfile2)
                                If (theurgy_data(0).type < 9) And (player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).direction = 5) Then
                                    player_battle_sprite(battle_enemy_idx).direction = 6
                                End If
                                actionParam2 = 3
                                npc_dir_frame = 0
                                If theurgy_data(0).type = 9 Then
                                    tmp = magicSubfile2
                                    npc_dir_frame = -1
                                    Call cast_theurgy_anim(equipID)
                                    actionParam2 = 9
                                End If
                                thyrgyResult6 = thyrgyResult6 \ 4
                                Call calc_display_theurgy(thyrgyResult6, equipID, targetIdx, actionParam2)
                                Call apply_enemy_poison_damage()
                                Call show_enemy_damage(thyrgyType)
                                If thyrgyType Then
                                    Call enemy_hit_reaction(5)
                                End If
                            Else
                                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 2)
                                Call play_sound(0, 30)
                                For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                                    If player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 0 Then
                                        player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 5
                                    End If
                                Next
                                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 7)
                            End If
                            For i = 0 To battle_action_queue
                                If battle_action_queue_ext(i) < 10 Then
                                    battle_action_queue_ext(i) = -1
                                End If
                            Next
                        Case 8
                            prevRoleIdx = 0
                            For i = 0 To team_number
                                hasEffect = calc_sprite_y_offset(4, i) + enemy_runtime_data(i).dexterity
                                If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
                                    prevRoleIdx = prevRoleIdx + hasEffect
                                End If
                            Next
                            rng2 = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
                            hasEffect = CSng (get_player_attribute_total(21, party(battle_enemy_idx).role)) >= (CSng (prevRoleIdx) * rng2)
                            If surpriseFlag = 0 Then
                                hasEffect = 0
                            End If
                            Call flee_from_battle(hasEffect, battle_enemy_idx)
                        Case 9
                            menuResult2 = random_alive_party_member()
                            If menuResult2 <> battle_enemy_idx Then
                                Call player_attack_player(menuResult2, battle_enemy_idx)
                            End If
                        Case 10
                            Call player_attack_execute(roleID, battle_enemy_idx)
                    End Select
                    If count_alive_enemies() < thyrgyResult5 Then
                        npc_sprite_num = -1
                        Call play_sound(0, battle_enemy_hp)
                    End If
                End If
            End If
L_00420322:
            For i = 0 To 4
                battle_enemy_data_ext(i) = enemy_battle_data(i).hp
            Next
            If npc_sprite_num Then
                Call play_rng_effect(65, 1)
                npc_sprite_num = 0
                For i = 0 To 11
                    damage_numbers(i).timer = 0
                Next
            End If
            If npc_dir_frame Then
                Call load_battle_sprites()
                npc_dir_frame = 0
                Call play_theurgy_rng_anim()
            End If
            If (count_alive_enemies() > 0) And magic_select_idx Then
                For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
                    If player_battle_sprite(i).direction >= 5 Then
                        player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 0
                        battle_sprite_data(i) = player_battle_sprite(i).origX
                        battle_sprite_data_ext(i) = player_battle_sprite(i).origY
                    End If
                Next
                Call adjust_battle_sprite_pos()
                magic_select_idx = 0
            End If
            Call update_player_battle_status()
            If check_party_alive() = 0 Then
                battleResult = 1
                GoTo L_00420C0E
            End If
            If count_alive_enemies() <= 0 Then
                battleResult = 3
                GoTo L_00420C0E
            End If
            If battle_action_param Then
                battleResult = battle_action_param
                GoTo L_00420C0E
            End If
        Next
        thyrgyType = 2
        For i = 0 To team_number
            For j = 0 To 15
                If enemy_status(i, j) > 0 Then
                    enemy_status(i, j) = enemy_status(i, j) - 1
                End If
            Next
            battle_enemy_data_ext(i) = enemy_battle_data(i).hp
            If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
                For j = 0 To 15
                    If enemy_poison_status(i, j).poisonID > 0 Then
                        Call process_Script(enemy_poison_status(i, j).poisonScript, i)
                    End If
                Next
                hpLost = battle_enemy_data_ext(i) - enemy_battle_data(i).hp
                If hpLost > 0 Then
                    Call add_damage_number(1, hpLost, enemy_battle_data(i).y - 110, enemy_battle_data(i).x)
                    thyrgyType = 10
                End If
                If enemy_battle_data(i).hp <= 0 Then
                    npc_sprite_num = -1
                End If
            End If
        Next
        For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            For j = 0 To 15
                If player_status(i, j) > 0 Then
                    player_status(i, j) = player_status(i, j) - 1
                End If
            Next
            k = party(i).role
            damage_target_hp = playerRoles(k, 9)
            battle_select_idx = playerRoles(k, 10)
            If damage_target_hp > 0 Then
                player_status(i, 4) = 0
                For j = 0 To 15
                    If poison_status(i, j).poisonID > 0 Then
                        Call process_Script(poison_status(i, j).poisonScript, i)
                    End If
                Next
                hpLost = damage_target_hp - playerRoles(k, 9)
                If hpLost > 0 Then
                    Call add_damage_number(1, hpLost, player_battle_sprite(i).y - 70, player_battle_sprite(i).x)
                    thyrgyType = 10
                End If
                hpLost = battle_select_idx - playerRoles(k, 10)
                If hpLost > 0 Then
                    Call add_damage_number(2, hpLost, player_battle_sprite(i).y - 62, player_battle_sprite(i).x)
                    thyrgyType = 10
                End If
            End If
            battle_role_data_ext(i).savedHP = playerRoles(k, 9)
            battle_role_data_ext(i).savedMP = playerRoles(k, 10)
            playerRoles(k, 9) = damage_target_hp
            playerRoles(k, 10) = battle_select_idx
        Next
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, thyrgyType)
        For i = 0 To 4
            battle_enemy_data_ext(i) = enemy_battle_data(i).hp
        Next
        For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            k = party(i).role
            playerRoles(k, 9) = battle_role_data_ext(i).savedHP
            playerRoles(k, 10) = battle_role_data_ext(i).savedMP
        Next
        If battle_select_max > 0 Then
            battle_select_max = battle_select_max - 1
            If battle_select_max = 0 Then
                npc_sprite_num = 1
            End If
        End If
        If npc_sprite_num Then
            Call play_rng_effect(65, 1)
            npc_sprite_num = 0
        End If
        Call PAL_ReadKey(key_state_array(0))
        If auto_battle_flag <= 2 Then
            If (key_state_array(1) > 0) Or (key_state_array(82) > 0) Then
                auto_battle_flag = 0
            End If
        End If
        Call DoEvents_check_exit()
    Loop
L_00420B20:
    For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        battle_role_data_ext(i).savedHP = playerRoles(party(i).role, 9)
        battle_role_data_ext(i).savedMP = playerRoles(party(i).role, 10)
    Next
    Call process_Script(objects(itemObjID).data2, battle_role_action(battle_enemy_idx).target)
    thyrgyType = 0
    Call show_party_hp_mp_change(thyrgyType)
    If thyrgyType Then
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 7)
        Call update_player_battle_status()
    End If
    Return
L_00420C0E:
    If battleResult = 1 Then
        Call update_player_battle_status()
        Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
    End If
    If battleResult = 3 Then
        If (battle_action_type > 0) Or (battle_max_hp_sum > 0) Then
            If surpriseFlag Then
                hasEffect = 3
            Else
                hasEffect = 2
            End If
            Call play_all_kinds_music(0, hasEffect)
            Call show_money(battle_action_type)
            Call calc_display_exp(battle_max_hp_sum)
        End If
        For i = 0 To team_number
            Call process_Script(enemy_battle_data(i).battleStartScript, i)
        Next
        For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
            k = party(i).role
            For j = 0 To 19
                If data_levelup_magic(k, j).magic > 0 Then
                    If data_levelup_magic(k, j).level <= playerRoles(k, 6) Then
                        Call add_magic_to_player(-1, data_levelup_magic(k, j).magic, k)
                    End If
                End If
            Next
            playerRoles(k, 9) = playerRoles(k, 9) + ((playerRoles(k, 7) - playerRoles(k, 9)) \ 2)
            playerRoles(k, 10) = playerRoles(k, 10) + ((playerRoles(k, 8) - playerRoles(k, 10)) \ 2)
        Next
    End If
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(75, enemy_battle_data(0).enemyID)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(120, player_battle_sprite(0).spriteNum)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(160, enemy_poison_status(0, 0).poisonID)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(160, poison_status(0, 0).poisonID)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(80, player_status(0, 0))
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(80, enemy_status(0, 0))
    auto_battle_flag = 0
    redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 3
    retval = battleResult
    Call PAL_ClearTree()
    Call PAL_FlushDSound()
