    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub process_scripts(ByRef eventObjID, ByRef scriptEntry, ByRef opcode, ByRef operand1, ByRef operand2, ByRef operand3)
    Call DoEvents_check_exit()
    If opcode <= 10 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If eventObjID <= 4 Then
        roleID = party(eventObjID).role
    End If
    opcodeCopy = opcode
    If opcodeCopy = 11 Then
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction = 0
        Call npc_walk_one_step(2, eventObjID)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 12 Then
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction = 1
        Call npc_walk_one_step(2, eventObjID)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 13 Then
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction = 2
        Call npc_walk_one_step(2, eventObjID)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 14 Then
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction = 3
        Call npc_walk_one_step(2, eventObjID)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 15 Then
        If operand1 >= 0 Then
            npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction = operand1
        End If
        If operand2 >= 0 Then
            npc_display_data(eventObjID).currentFrame = operand2
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 16 Then
        stepSize = 3
L_00421140:
        targetX = ((operand1 + operand1) + operand3) * 16
        targetY = ((operand2 + operand2) + operand3) * 8
        tmp2 = targetX - npc_display_data(eventObjID).x
        tmp3 = targetY - npc_display_data(eventObjID).y
        If (Abs (tmp2) >= (stepSize + stepSize)) Or (Abs (tmp3) >= stepSize) Then
            Call PAL_ExTF(tmp3, tmp2, npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction)
            Call npc_walk_one_step(stepSize, eventObjID)
        Else
            npc_display_data(eventObjID).x = targetX
            npc_display_data(eventObjID).y = targetY
        End If
        If (npc_display_data(eventObjID).x <> targetX) Or (npc_display_data(eventObjID).y <> targetY) Then
            scriptEntry = scriptEntry - 1
            Exit Sub
        End If
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).currentFrame = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 17 Then
        If (eventObjID And 1) = mutex_shaking Then
            stepSize = 2
            GoTo L_00421140
        End If
        scriptEntry = scriptEntry - 1
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 18 Then
        If operand1 <= 0 Then
            eventObjIdx = eventObjID
        Else
            eventObjIdx = operand1 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
        End If
        If (eventObjIdx > 0) And (eventObjIdx <= curr_scene_event_count) Then
            npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).x = party_abs_x + operand2
            npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).y = party_abs_y + operand3
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 19 Then
        If operand1 <= 0 Then
            eventObjIdx = eventObjID
        Else
            eventObjIdx = operand1 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
        End If
        If (eventObjIdx > 0) And (eventObjIdx <= curr_scene_event_count) Then
            npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).x = operand2
            npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).y = operand3
            Exit Sub
        End If
        save_temp_buf(0) = operand2
        save_temp_buf(1) = operand3
        Call PAL_CopyMem(4, save_temp_buf(0), events(((operand1 - 1) * 32) + 2))
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 20 Then
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).currentFrame = operand1
        npc_display_data(eventObjID).direction = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 21 Then
        RPG_team_direction = operand1
        party(operand3).frame = (RPG_team_direction * 3) + operand2
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 22 Then
        If operand1 = 0 Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If operand1 < 0 Then
            eventObjIdx = eventObjID
        Else
            eventObjIdx = operand1 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
        End If
        If (eventObjIdx > 0) And (eventObjIdx <= curr_scene_event_count) Then
            npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).direction = operand2
            npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).currentFrame = operand3
            Exit Sub
        End If
        save_temp_buf(0) = operand2
        save_temp_buf(1) = operand3
        Call PAL_CopyMem(4, save_temp_buf(0), events(((operand1 - 1) * 32) + 20))
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 23 Then
        equipment_effect(roleID, operand1, operand2) = operand3
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 24 Then
        For i = 17 To 30
            equipment_effect(roleID, operand1, i) = 0
        Next
        coop_magic_tmp = playerRoles(roleID, operand1)
        playerRoles(roleID, operand1) = operand2
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 25 Then
        If operand3 > 0 Then
            roleID = operand3 - 1
        End If
        playerRoles(roleID, operand1) = playerRoles(roleID, operand1) + operand2
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 26 Then
        If operand3 <= 0 Then
            palDst = operand1
            If palDst = 1 Then
                player_battle_sprite(eventObjID).spriteNum = operand2
                Exit Sub
            End If
            If palDst = 65 Then
                player_battle_sprite(eventObjID).equipID = operand2
                Exit Sub
            End If
            playerRoles(roleID, operand1) = operand2
            Exit Sub
        End If
        playerRoles(operand3 - 1, operand1) = operand2
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 27 Then
        If operand1 Then
            loopStart = 0
            loopEnd = RPG_team_number
        Else
            loopStart = eventObjID
            loopEnd = eventObjID
        End If
        totalChange = 0
        For j = loopStart To loopEnd
            eventObjIdx = party(j).role
            If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) > 0 Then
                oldVal = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9)
                playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) + operand2
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) < 0 Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) = 0
                End If
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) > playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 7) Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 7)
                End If
                totalChange = totalChange + Abs (playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) - oldVal)
            End If
        Next
        redraw_hp_mp_flag = totalChange <> 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 28 Then
        If operand1 Then
            loopStart = 0
            loopEnd = RPG_team_number
        Else
            loopStart = eventObjID
            loopEnd = eventObjID
        End If
        totalChange = 0
        For j = loopStart To loopEnd
            eventObjIdx = party(j).role
            If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) > 0 Then
                oldVal = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10)
                playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) + operand2
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) < 0 Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) = 0
                End If
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) > playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 8) Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 8)
                End If
                totalChange = totalChange + Abs (playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) - oldVal)
            End If
        Next
        redraw_hp_mp_flag = totalChange <> 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 29 Then
        If operand1 Then
            loopStart = 0
            loopEnd = RPG_team_number
        Else
            loopStart = eventObjID
            loopEnd = eventObjID
        End If
        totalChange = 0
        For j = loopStart To loopEnd
            eventObjIdx = party(j).role
            If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) > 0 Then
                oldVal = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9)
                oldMP = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10)
                playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) + operand2
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) < 0 Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) = 0
                End If
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) > playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 7) Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 7)
                End If
                playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) + operand2
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) < 0 Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) = 0
                End If
                If playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) > playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 8) Then
                    playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) = playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 8)
                End If
                totalChange = (totalChange + Abs (playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 9) - oldVal)) + Abs (playerRoles(eventObjIdx, 10) - oldMP)
            End If
        Next
        redraw_hp_mp_flag = totalChange <> 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 30 Then
        If (operand1 < 0) And ((RPG_money + operand1) < 0) Then
            scriptEntry = operand2 - 1
            Exit Sub
        End If
        RPG_money = RPG_money + operand1
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 31 Then

        If poisonSlot > 1 Then
            scriptEntry = operand1 - 1
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 146 Then
        If flag_battling Then
            If operand1 > 0 Then
                loopRoleID = operand1 - 1
                player_battle_sprite(loopRoleID).direction = 6
                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
                Call player_attack_anim(0, loopRoleID)
            End If
            Call animate_battle_sprites(enemy_pos_count, 0)
            Call play_rng_effect(65, 1)
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 147 Then
        Call cross_fade_out(operand1)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 148 Then
        If operand1 = 0 Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If operand1 < 0 Then
            eventObjIdx = eventObjID
        Else
            eventObjIdx = operand1 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
        End If
        If (eventObjIdx <= 0) Or (eventObjIdx > curr_scene_event_count) Then
            eventObjIdx = 0
            Call PAL_CopyMem(32, events((operand1 - 1) * 32), npc_display_data(0).vanishTime)
        End If
        If npc_display_data(eventObjIdx).state = operand2 Then
            scriptEntry = operand3 - 1
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 149 Then
        If RPG_curr_scene = operand1 Then
            scriptEntry = operand2 - 1
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 150 Then
        Call play_opening_anim()
        redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 2
        curr_scene_id_cache = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 151 Then
        stepSize = 8
        GoTo L_00423634
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 152 Then
        RPG_other_peoples = 0
        If operand1 > 0 Then
            RPG_other_peoples = 1
            party(RPG_team_number + 1).role = operand1
        End If
        If operand2 > 0 Then
            RPG_other_peoples = 2
            party(RPG_team_number + 2).role = operand2
        End If
        If RPG_other_peoples > 0 Then
            Call load_team_mgo()
            Call set_team_draw()
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 153 Then
        If operand1 < 0 Then
            operand1 = RPG_curr_scene
            scenes(operand1).mapNum = operand2
            Call get_scene_map_source(operand1)
            Exit Sub
        End If
        scenes(operand1).mapNum = operand2
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 154 Then
        If (operand1 = 0) Or (operand2 = 0) Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        eventIdx1 = operand1 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
        eventIdx2 = operand2 - scenes(RPG_curr_scene).eventObjectIndex
        If (eventIdx1 > 0) And (eventIdx1 <= curr_scene_event_count) Then
            For j = eventIdx1 To eventIdx2
                npc_display_data(j).state = operand3
            Next
            Exit Sub
        End If
        For j = operand1 To operand2
            Call PAL_CopyMem(2, operand3, events(((j - 1) * 32) + 12))
        Next
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 155 Then
        multi_event_state = operand1
        relative_viewport_x = 0
        relative_viewport_y = 0
        If operand1 = 0 Then
            curr_scene_id_cache = 0
            Call get_scene_map_source(RPG_curr_scene)
            Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
            multi_event_state = 0
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If operand2 < 0 Then
            Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, global_buf_1(0), screen_buf(0))
            curr_scene_id_cache = 0
            Call PAL_CopyMem(draw_battle_row_sprite(), 64000, global_buf_1(0), mgo_frame_offsets(0))
            Call PAL_CopyMem(64000, screen_buf(0), global_buf_1(0))
            GoTo L_00426BDE
        End If
        filename = 'FBP.MKF'
        Call read_file_and_close(operand2, filename)
        If multi_event_state = 1 Then
            Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_1(0), global_buf_2(0))
            GoTo L_00426BDE
        End If
        curr_scene_id_cache = 0
        Call PAL_Unpak(draw_battle_row_sprite(), mgo_frame_offsets(0), global_buf_2(0))
L_00426BDE:
        If operand3 = 0 Then
            operand3 = 2
        End If
        If multi_event_state = 2 Then
            multi_event_param = operand3
            multi_event_param_2 = 0
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 156 Then
        If operand1 = 0 Then
            operand1 = 1
        End If
        stepSize = operand1
        If (count_alive_enemies() = 1) And (enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).hp > 1) Then
            For j = 0 To 4
                If (stepSize > 0) And (enemy_battle_data(j).hp <= 0) Then
                    stepSize = stepSize - 1
                    CopyBytes enemy_battle_data(j), enemy_battle_data(eventObjID), len=30
                    CopyBytes enemy_runtime_data(j), enemy_runtime_data(eventObjID), len=70
                    battle_action_type = battle_action_type + enemy_runtime_data(j).cash
                    If enemy_runtime_data(j).exp > (32767 - battle_max_hp_sum) Then
                        battle_max_hp_sum = 32767
                    Else
                        battle_max_hp_sum = battle_max_hp_sum + enemy_runtime_data(j).exp
                    End If
                End If
            Next
            For j = 0 To 4
                If enemy_battle_data(j).hp > 0 Then
                    team_number = j
                    enemy_battle_data(j).hp = (enemy_battle_data(j).hp + operand1) \ (operand1 + 1)
                End If
            Next
            enemy_max_id = team_number + 1
            For j = 0 To team_number
                enemy_battle_data(j).origX = enemy_pos_data(team_number, j).x
                enemy_battle_data(j).origY = enemy_pos_data(team_number, j).y + enemy_runtime_data(j).yPosOffset
            Next
            For k = 1 To 10
                For j = 0 To team_number
                    enemy_battle_data(j).x = (enemy_battle_data(j).x + enemy_battle_data(j).origX) \ 2
                    enemy_battle_data(j).y = (enemy_battle_data(j).y + enemy_battle_data(j).origY) \ 2
                Next
                Call draw_battle_scene(0, 1)
            Next
            Call init_enemy_positions()
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If operand2 <> 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand2 - 1
            redraw_hp_mp_flag = 0
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 157 Then
        If count_alive_enemies() < enemy_max_id Then
            Call enemy_cast_anim(eventObjID)
            For j = 0 To team_number
                If (enemy_battle_data(j).objectID > 0) And (enemy_battle_data(j).hp <= 0) Then
                    Call load_enemy_data(enemy_battle_data(j).objectID, j)
                    Call clear_enemy_poison(j)
                    enemy_status(j, 1) = 1
                    player_hit_flags(j) = -1
                    enemy_battle_data(j).hp = enemy_runtime_data(j).health \ 2
                End If
                If operand1 = 0 Then
                    j = enemy_max_id
                End If
            Next
            Call load_enemy_sprites()
            operand1 = 70
            Call play_rng_effect(78, 2)
            For j = 0 To 4
                player_hit_flags(j) = 0
            Next
            Call play_rng_effect(42, 1)
            enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).direction = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).flag
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If operand2 <> 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand2 - 1
            redraw_hp_mp_flag = 0
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 158 Then
        If operand1 <= 0 Then
            operand1 = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).objectID
        End If
        If operand2 = 0 Then
            operand2 = 1
        End If
        stepSize = operand2
        If (count_alive_enemies() + operand2) <= enemy_max_id Then
            Call enemy_cast_anim(eventObjID)
            For j = 0 To 4
                If (stepSize > 0) And (enemy_battle_data(j).hp <= 0) Then
                    stepSize = stepSize - 1
                    Call load_enemy_data(operand1, j)
                    Call clear_enemy_poison(j)
                    enemy_status(j, 1) = 1
                    player_hit_flags(j) = -1
                End If
            Next
            savedDir = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).direction
            Call load_enemy_sprites()
            operand1 = 69
            enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).direction = savedDir
            Call play_sound(1, 212)
            Call play_rng_effect(78, 1)
            For j = 0 To 4
                player_hit_flags(j) = 0
            Next
            Call play_rng_effect(42, 1)
            enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).direction = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).flag
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If operand3 <> 0 Then
            scriptEntry = operand3 - 1
            redraw_hp_mp_flag = 0
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 159 Then
        If operand1 > 0 Then
            Call enemy_cast_anim(eventObjID)
            battle_max_hp_sum = battle_max_hp_sum - enemy_runtime_data(eventObjID).exp
            battle_action_type = battle_action_type - enemy_runtime_data(eventObjID).cash
            savedHP = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).hp
            savedUseScript = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).useScript
            savedEndScript = enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).battleEndScript
            Call load_enemy_data(operand1, eventObjID)
            enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).hp = savedHP
            enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).useScript = savedUseScript
            enemy_battle_data(eventObjID).battleEndScript = savedEndScript
            Call load_enemy_sprites()
            If operand2 = 0 Then
                operand2 = 47
            End If
            Call play_sound(0, operand2)
            Call play_rng_effect(72, 1)
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 160 Then
        Call PAL_PlayAvi(Me.method_0038(rng, vb_com_object_1.f0000), 1, 4, rng)
        Call PAL_PlayAvi(Me.method_0038(rng, vb_com_object_1.f0000), 1, 5, rng)
        Call PAL_PlayAvi(Me.method_0038(rng, vb_com_object_1.f0000), 1, 6, rng)
        Call release_resources_exit()
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 161 Then
        For j = 1 To RPG_team_number
            party(j).x = team_abstract_x
            party(j).y = team_abstract_y - 2
            trail(j).x = party_abs_x
            trail(j).y = party_abs_y
        Next
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 162 Then
        rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        scriptEntry = CSng (scriptEntry) + Int (rng * CSng (operand1))
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 163 Then
        If operand3 <= 1 Then
            operand3 = operand3 Xor 1
        End If
        Call play_cd(operand3, operand2, operand1)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 164 Then
        If operand3 <= 0 Then
            operand3 = 10
        End If
        Call scroll_scene_with_fbp(operand3, operand2, operand1)
        redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 2
        curr_scene_id_cache = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 165 Then
        If operand3 <= 0 Then
            operand3 = 10
        End If
        Call fade_in_pic(operand3, operand2, operand1)
        redraw_flag = redraw_flag Or 2
        curr_scene_id_cache = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 166 Then
        Call PAL_PushScreen(global_buf_1(0))
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If opcodeCopy = 167 Then
        dialog_type = 9
        dialog_width = 72
        dialog_height = eventObjID
        dialog_x = 60
        If operand1 Then
            dialog_x = operand1
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub
